### [102] [Polyconvexity for incompressible inversion-symmetric energies of Valanis-Landel type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18660)
*Ionel-Dumitrel Ghiba,Maximilian P. Wollner,Patrizio Neff*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究不可压各向同性超弹性能量 $W_\psi(F)=\sum_i \psi(|\log\lambda_i|)$ 的多凸性，证明若 $g(s)=\psi(\mathrm{arcosh}(s/2))$ 有凸且非减的延拓 $\bar g$，则 $W_\psi$ 是显式多凸泛函在 $\mathrm{SL}(3)$ 上的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对不可压各向同性弹性材料，能量泛函的多凸性是保证变分问题弱下半连续性及存在性的重要条件；论文旨在为一大类仅依赖奇异值对数（即对数应变）的能量给出可验证的多凸性判据，并澄清"关于 $\log\lambda_i$ 凸"这一常见直觉是否足以保证多凸性。

Method: 关键恒等式 $\nu_i(F+\mathrm{Cof}\,F)=\lambda_i+\lambda_i^{-1}$（在 $\mathrm{SL}(3)$ 上至多相差置换）将 $W_\psi$ 重写为 $F+\mathrm{Cof}\,F$ 的奇异值函数，再结合 Ball 关于奇异值函数的凸性定理构造显式多凸延拓 $F\mapsto\sum_i \bar g(\nu_i(F+\mathrm{Cof}\,F))$；同时给出沿 $\mathrm{SL}(3)$ 内秩一方向的直接证明，并推导一维微分充分条件。

Result: 在 $g$ 存在凸且非减延拓的条件下，$W_\psi$ 为 $\mathrm{SL}(3)$ 上多凸能量之限制；特别地 $F\mapsto\sum_i e^{\log^2\lambda_i}$ 在 $\mathrm{SL}(3)$ 上秩一凸并具有所述多凸延拓；另一方面，纯剪计算表明仅对 $\log\lambda_i$ 标量凸并不充分，二次 Hencky 能量 $\sum_i\log^2\lambda_i$ 在 $\mathrm{SL}(3)$ 上不是秩一凸。

Conclusion: 论文给出不可压各向同性对数型能量的多凸性充分条件及显式多凸延拓，并通过反例（二次 Hencky 能量非秩一凸）指出关于 $\log\lambda_i$ 的标量凸性不足以推出多凸性，从而厘清了该类能量的凸性层级关系。

Abstract: {Let $λ_i = ν_i(F)$ denote the three singular values of the deformation gradient $F \in {\rm GL}^+(3)$.} We consider the family of incompressible isotropic energies $
  W_ψ(F)=\sum_i ψ\left(|\!\logλ_i|\right)$ with $ψ:[0,\infty)\mapsto\mathbb{R}$. Set $g(s)=ψ\left({\rm arcosh}\frac{s}{2}\right)$ for all $s\geq2$. If $g$ has a convex and non-decreasing extension $\bar g$ to $[0,\infty)$, then $W_ψ$ is the restriction to ${\rm SL}(3)$ of the explicit polyconvex function \ {$
  F\mapsto\sum_i \bar g\left(ν_i(F+{\rm Cof} F)\right). $} The proof uses the identity $ν_i(F+{\rm Cof} F)=λ_i+λ_i^{-1}$, up to permutation, on ${\rm SL}(3)$ and Ball's convexity theorem for functions of the singular values. We also give a direct proof along rank-one lines contained in ${\rm SL}(3)$ and derive a convenient one-dimensional differential sufficient condition. In particular, $
  F\mapsto\sum_i e^{\log^2\!λ_i} $ is rank-one convex on ${\rm SL}(3)$ and possesses the stated polyconvex extension. A simple-shear computation shows that scalar convexity in $\log λ_i$ alone is insufficient; the quadratic Hencky energy $\sum_i \log^2λ_i$ is not rank-one convex on ${\rm SL}(3)$.

</details>


### [103] [The nonlinear Schrödinger equation on products of $\mathbb{R}^N$ and compact metric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18698)
*Nicola Soave,Gianmaria Verzini,Lorenzo Villata*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究 $\mathbb{R}^N\times\mathcal{G}$（欧氏空间与紧致度量图的乘积）上的定态聚焦 NLS 方程，在质量约束变分框架下建立泛函分析框架，并刻画基态存在性与质量阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该乘积结构是一类新型混合结构：其面均为 $(N+1)$ 维并沿余维一的界面粘合，能量空间是真正的 Sobolev 空间，同时图的度量与拓扑均进入变分问题，因此需要发展新的泛函框架并研究质量约束下基态的存在与阈值行为。

Method: 建立泛函框架：给出 $H^1(\mathbb{R}^N\times\mathcal{G})$ 的两种等价描述；在每个变量上引入部分重排（partial rearrangements）及相应的 Pólya–Szegő 不等式；证明局部化形式的 Gagliardo–Nirenberg 不等式，并与 $\mathbb{R}^{N+1}$ 及半空间的最优常数作比较。随后分析质量约束极小化问题，利用图覆盖（cycle covering）与端边（terminal edge）结构分析临界质量阈值，并通过 Kirchhoff Laplacian 的第一非零特征值刻画半平凡解的局部极小性阈值。

Result: 当 $2<p<2_*:=2+4/(N+1)$ 时，对任意质量基态存在。在临界指数 $p=2_*$ 时，基态存在于一个依赖图的阈值之下，该阈值位于欧氏临界质量的一半与完整欧氏临界质量之间；当图存在 cycle covering 时取到完整值，存在 terminal edge 时阈值恰为一半，且两种情况阈值均为 sharp。当 $2_*<p<2+4/N$ 时全局极小元不存在，但在进一步的质量阈值下证明了局部极小元的存在性并给出显式下界。此外刻画了维数跨越现象：低于某临界质量时极小元不依赖于图变量，并以 Kirchhoff Laplacian 的第一非零特征值给出半平凡解为局部极小元的阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作为 $\mathbb{R}^N\times\mathcal{G}$ 上的质量约束 NLS 建立了系统的变分框架与不等式工具，完整刻画了不同指数区间内基态与局部极小元的存在性及质量阈值，并揭示了由图拓扑与度量控制的维数跨越与阈值二分现象。

Abstract: We study the stationary focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation on the product $\mathbb{R}^N \times \mathcal{G}$ of the Euclidean space with a compact metric graph, in the mass-constrained variational setting. Such a product is a hybrid structure of a new type: all its faces are $(N+1)$-dimensional and are glued along interfaces of codimension one, so that the energy space is a genuine Sobolev space, while both the metric and the topology of the graph enter the variational problem. We first develop the functional framework, giving two equivalent descriptions of $H^1(\mathbb{R}^N \times \mathcal{G})$, introducing partial rearrangements in each of the two variables together with the corresponding Pólya--Szegő inequalities, and proving Gagliardo--Nirenberg inequalities in a localized form, with a comparison of the optimal constants with those of $\mathbb{R}^{N+1}$ and of the half-space. We then study the mass-constrained problem. Ground states exist for every mass when $2<p<2_*:=2+4/(N+1)$. At the critical exponent $p=2_*$, they exist below a graph-dependent threshold lying between one half of the Euclidean critical mass and the full Euclidean critical mass. The latter value is attained when the graph admits a cycle covering, whereas the threshold is exactly halved in the presence of a terminal edge. In both cases, the threshold is sharp. For $2_*<p<2+4/N$ global minimizers do not exist, but we prove the existence of local minimizers below a further mass threshold, for which we give an explicit lower bound. Finally, we describe the dimensional crossover: below a critical mass the minimizers do not depend on the graph variable, and we characterize the threshold below which the semi-trivial solution is a local minimizer in terms of the first nonzero eigenvalue of the Kirchhoff Laplacian on $\mathcal{G}$.

</details>


### [104] [Determining Schrodinger Potentials on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18711)
*Lu Chen,Yan Jiang,Hongyu Liu,Longyue Tao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带边Riemann流形上定态Schrödinger方程势函数的逆问题，利用Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射给出两个唯一可识别性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带边Riemann流形上由Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射确定Schrödinger方程势函数是一个具有挑战性的公开逆问题，本文旨在推进该问题的解决。

Method: 以Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射为数据，分别在同两个假设下进行理论分析：(1) Riemann度量接近Euclidean度量，接近程度由施加于势函数的相对Fourier条件量化；(2) 流形为乘积型且势函数可加分离。

Result: 在两个假设下分别导出了唯一可识别性结果：一是度量接近Euclidean且势函数满足相对Fourier条件的情形；二是乘积型流形且势函数可加分离的情形。

Conclusion: 结果与技术论证均为新的，推动了这一困难公开逆问题的研究进展。

Abstract: We consider the inverse problem of determining a potential for the stationary Schrödinger equation on certain Riemannian manifolds with boundary from the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map. We derive two unique identifiability results. The first assumes that the Riemannian metric is close to the Euclidean metric, with the closeness quantified by a relative Fourier condition imposed on the potential function. The second assumes that the manifold is of product type and that the potential function is additively separable. Both the results and the technical arguments are new, contributing to the advancement of this challenging open inverse problem.

</details>


### [105] [Existence of flat blowups at boundary points of anisotropic minimizing hypercurrents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18838)
*Michael Novack,Reinaldo Resende*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究 $\mathbb{R}^{m+1}$ 中几乎极小各向异性流在可微边界点处的平坦爆破（flat blowup）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对各向异性极小流（anisotropic currents）的正则性理论，已有结果多要求被积函数具有较强正则性。本文旨在放宽正则性假设，考察当被积函数仅对空间变量连续、对法向变量为 $C^{2,\rm Dini}$ 时，几乎极小流在边界点附近的渐近行为。

Method: 考虑 $\mathbb{R}^{m+1}$ 中的 $m$ 维流，其能量泛函的被积函数在空间变量上连续、在法向变量上为 $C^{2,\rm Dini}$ 类；运用爆破（blowup）分析与单调性/紧性论证，结合边界可微性假设，构造并分析缩放后的极限流。

Result: 证明了若此类几乎极小各向异性流的边界可微，则在每个边界点处该流存在平坦爆破。

Conclusion: 该结果将各向异性极小流的平坦爆破结论推广到更弱正则的被积函数情形，为边界正则性研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We consider $m$-dimensional currents in $\mathbb{R}^{m+1}$ that almost minimize an anisotropic energy whose integrand is continuous in the space variable and $C^{2,{\rm Dini}}$ in the normal variable. We prove that if the boundary of such a current is differentiable, then the current admits a flat blowup at every boundary point.

</details>


### [106] [Classification of solutions to the Liouville equation with a nonlinear Neumann boundary condition and applications to sharp inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18762)
*Xiaohan Cai,Abdolhakim Shouman*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带非线性Neumann边界条件的Liouville方程，利用边界适配的P函数方法对所有光滑解进行分类，并由此建立Sobolev迹型不等式及其亏量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liouville方程及其边值问题在几何分析与不等式理论中具有重要地位，而带Neumann边界条件的版本涉及所有符号的K与k，符号变化使解的构造与分类复杂，已有方法多依赖符号限制。作者希望给出统一处理框架并对解完全分类，同时导出新的Sobolev迹型不等式。

Method: 采用边界适配的P函数（P-function）方法，在单位圆盘上对内部方程-Δu=Ke^{2u}与边界方程∂u/∂ν+λ=ke^u联立处理，通过构造合适的辅助量并利用最大值原理，将K和k的所有符号情形纳入统一分析。

Result: 建立了该边值问题所有光滑解的完整分类；作为应用，得到一族尖锐的Sobolev迹型不等式，涵盖经典Lebedev–Milin不等式，并给出相应的亏量估计。

Conclusion: 边界适配P函数方法为带非线Neumann边界条件的Liouville方程提供了统一且有效的分析途径，所得分类结果可自然导出含亏量的Sobolev迹型不等式，推广了经典结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Liouville equation with a nonlinear Neumann boundary condition \begin{equation*}
  \begin{cases} -Δu = Ke^{2u} & \text{in } \mathbb{B}^{2},\\[2mm] \dfrac{\partial u}{\partial ν}+λ= ke^{u} & \text{on } \partial\mathbb{B}^{2}, \end{cases} \end{equation*} where $K,k\in\mathbb{R}$, $λ\in(0,1]$ are constants, and $ν$ denotes the outward unit normal on $ \partial\mathbb{B}^{2}$. We establish a classification of all smooth solutions to the equation. Our approach is a boundary-adapted P-function method which treats all signs of $K$ and $k$ within a unified framework. As applications of the classification result, we establish a family of sharp Sobolev-trace-type inequalities encompassing the classical Lebedev--Milin inequality, together with a corresponding deficit estimate.

</details>


### [107] [Layered Dissipative Neural Fields: Bounding the Dimension of the Population Activity Manifold with Infinitely Many Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18774)
*Matthias Rakotomalala,Johannes Zimmer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究分层神经场模型（半线性高阶抛物型方程组），证明高阶耗散诱导谱间隙，使半群具有惯性流形，并给出其维数上界及显式标度关系；进一步证明普适逼近性质（稳定模式表达与输入依赖的有限时域动力学表达），并给出行波与非平凡稳态解存在的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为经验上提出的"神经流形"概念（大规模神经群体集体活动位于低维流形上）提供数学建模基础，同时刻画间隙连接活动调控所引入的高阶耗散对系统动力学复杂度的影响。

Method: 研究对象为无穷神经元群体组织成有限层堆叠的神经场模型，写成半线性高阶抛物型方程系统；利用高阶耗散项诱导的谱间隙，分析相应半群的惯性流形存在性，并估计其维数；此外采用逼近论方法证明稳定模式表达与输入依赖的动力学表达，并推导行波与稳态解的存在性条件。

Result: 证明该半群存在有限维惯性流形，可吸引无穷维系统所有轨道；给出流形维数的上界，并显式刻划其随耗散阶数与强度、泄漏率及连接算子范数的标度关系；证明稳定模式的普适表达性与有限时域内输入依赖的动力学表达性；给出行波与非平凡稳态解存在的充分条件。

Conclusion: 高阶耗散通过谱间隙机制将无穷维神经场动力学有效约化到有限维惯性流形，为"神经流形"假说提供了严格的数学支撑，并揭示了模型在模式表达与动力学表达上的普适性及行波、稳态解的存在机制。

Abstract: We consider a class of neural field models describing the macroscopic activity of an infinite population of neurons organized into finitely many stacked layers, written as a system of semi-linear higher-order parabolic equations. For this class of biologically inspired equations, we show that the higher-order dissipation, modeling gap junction activity regulation, induces a spectral gap ensuring that the associated semigroup possesses an inertial manifold, that is, a finite-dimensional manifold attracting all orbits of the infinite-dimensional system of neural activity. Our main result is an upper bound on the dimension of this manifold, giving its explicit scaling in the dissipation order and strength, the leaking rate, and the norm of the connection operator. We interpret this as a step toward the mathematical modeling of the empirically motivated concept of neural manifold, namely the idea that the collective activity of a large neural population lies on a low-dimensional manifold. For this class of equations, we further prove universal approximation properties, namely stationary pattern expressivity in terms of the input and input-dependent dynamical expressivity over finite time horizons. Finally, we provide sufficient conditions for the existence of traveling waves and nontrivial stationary solutions.

</details>


### [108] [Reaction-diffusion equations in periodic media: spreading speeds and spreading sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18795)
*Hongjun Guo,François Hamel,Luca Rossi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究全空间 R^N 中空间周期反应-扩散-对流方程在无界初始支集下的解，证明了扩展集的存在性及扩展速度的变分公式，并给出了上水平集位置的估计，适用于 Fisher-KPP、ignition 和 bistable 反应类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展速度与扩展集理论多针对有界初始支集，而无界初始支集下解的大时间行为尚缺乏一般性刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过引入"回缩解"（retracting solutions）的上界估计，结合变分方法，证明扩展集的存在性并推导扩展速度的变分公式；将无界初始支集解的上水平集位置用初始支集与有界初始支集解的扩展集进行估计。

Result: 证明了大时间下扩展集的存在性及扩展速度的变分公式；建立了无界初始支集解上水平集位置的估计；结果统一适用于 Fisher-KPP、ignition 和 bistable 反应；具体应用于 V 形和 Λ 形初始支集。

Conclusion: 该工作将扩展集理论推广至无界初始支集情形，给出了扩展速度的变分刻画与水平集位置估计，为周期介质中反应-扩散-对流方程的大时间传播行为提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We consider solutions of space-periodic reaction-diffusion-advection equations in the whole space R^N, with general unbounded initial support. We show the existence of spreading sets for the solutions at large time, and variational formulas for the spreading speeds. The location of the upper-level sets of a solution with unbounded initial support is estimated in terms of that initial support and the spreading set for the solutions with bounded initial supports. The results hold for the standard classes of Fisher-KPP, ignition, or bistable reactions. The general results apply to the cases of V-shaped and Lambda-shaped initial supports. The proofs rely in particular on upper estimates of what we call retracting solutions.

</details>


### [109] [A very short proof of a new energy identity for strong solutions to the Navier-Stokes system in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18808)
*Thomas Ruf*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 上不可压齐次 Navier-Stokes 系统在 $H^1_\sigma$ 初值下强解整体存在，核心是新的能量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决三维不可压 Navier-Stokes 方程强解的有限时间爆破问题，即证明 $H^1_\sigma$ 初值下强解整体存在。

Method: 基于材料导数与无散度约束的性质，构造新的能量泛函，并建立粘性矫顽性与扰动压力项之间的精细估计，排除 $L^r$（$r=2+2/\sqrt{3}$）范数的有限时间爆破。

Result: 证明 $H^1_\sigma$ 初值下强解整体存在；该估计不适用于仅具有标准能量结构的系统（如 Tao 的平均 Navier-Stokes 系统）；Leray-Hopf 弱解在 $u_0\in L^2_\sigma\cap L^3$ 时整体唯一；$L^2_\sigma$ 初值下唯一性破坏仅可能来自初始分支，且解在 $(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^3$ 上 $C^\infty$ 光滑；光滑快衰减初值下解在 $[0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^3$ 上 $C^\infty$ 光滑。

Conclusion: 建立了排除有限时间爆破的新能量估计，得到强解整体正则性及 Leray-Hopf 弱解的唯一性与光滑性结果。

Abstract: We prove that strong solutions to the incompressible, homogeneous Navier-Stokes system on $\mathbb{R}^3$ are global in time for initial data in the Sobolev space $H^1_σ(\mathbb{R}^3)$. The central ingredient of the proof is a new energy estimate that rules out finite-time blow-up in $L^r(\mathbb{R}^3)$ for $r = 2 + 2 / \sqrt{3}$. This estimate is based crucially on properties of the material derivative in combination with the divergence-free constraint, together with an intricate estimate that harnesses the delicate interplay between the viscous coercivity of the new energy functional and the control of a perturbing pressure term. Notably, the estimate does not carry over to systems that merely share the standard energy structure, such as T. Tao's averaged Navier-Stokes system. As a consequence of the new energy estimate, every Leray-Hopf weak solution $u$ to the homogeneous Navier-Stokes system is globally unique whenever the initial value $u(0, \cdot)$ belongs to $L^2_σ(\mathbb{R}^3) \cap L^3(\mathbb{R}^3)$. Even for $u(0, \cdot) \in L^2_σ(\mathbb{R}^3)$, the only possible non-uniqueness in the class of Leray-Hopf weak solutions is initial branching, and every such solution is $C^\infty$-smooth on $\left( 0, \infty \right) \times \mathbb{R}^3$. Moreover, if $u(0, \cdot)$ is smooth with derivatives of all orders decaying rapidly at infinity, we show that $u$ is in fact $C^\infty$-smooth on all of $\left[ 0, \infty \right) \times \mathbb{R}^3$.

</details>


### [110] [Integral Harnack inequality and expansion of positivity for fractional porous medium equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18809)
*Filippo Maria Cassanello,Filomena De Filippis,Calvin Stanko*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在最优尾部假设下，为快速扩散区制的分数阶多孔介质型方程的非负弱解建立了积分型Harnack不等式与正性扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶多孔介质型方程在快速扩散区制下的正则性理论尚不完善，尤其缺乏在最优（最弱）尾部假设下的积分型Harnack不等式与正性扩张结果。

Method: 针对分数阶多孔介质型方程的非负弱解，在快速扩散区制（fast diffusion regime）下，采用积分型Harnack不等式与正性扩张（expansion of positivity）技术，并在最优尾部假设下进行估计。

Result: 建立了积分型Harnack不等式以及正性扩张，且所依赖的尾部假设达到最优。

Conclusion: 该结果在最优尾部假设下推广了快速扩散区制分数阶多孔介质型方程弱解的正则性理论。

Abstract: We establish an integral Harnack inequality and expansion of positivity for nonnegative weak solutions to fractional porous medium-type equations in the fast diffusion regime, under optimal tail assumptions.

</details>


### [111] [Convergence of a multi-fluid scheme for the Vlasov--Poisson system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18810)
*Mehdi Badsi,Nicolas Crouseilles,Daniel Han-Kwan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 提出一维Vlasov-Poisson系统的多流体近似格式，适用于任意长时间区间，在低正则性下证明Wasserstein-1误差为O(h^{2/3})，从而证明无压Euler-Poisson与Vlasov-Poisson在网格趋于零时的一致性，并给出数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维Vlasov-Poisson系统的高效数值近似需求，尤其在长时间区间与低正则性解情形下；同时需要严格刻画无压Euler-Poisson与Vlasov-Poisson之间的关系。

Method: 引入适用于任意长时间区间的新型多流体近似格式；以速度网格尺寸h为参数进行离散化，在低正则性假设下用Wasserstein-1距离建立误差估计。

Result: 在Wasserstein-1距离下得到误差估计，收敛阶为O(h^{2/3})，h为速度网格尺寸；由此证明当h→0时，无压Euler-Poisson与Vlasov-Poisson系统之间的一致（consistency）关系。数值算例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该多流体近似格式在长时间区间上对低正则性解有效，误差阶为O(h^{2/3})，从理论上建立了两类系统的一致性，并通过数值实验证实了其效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel multi-fluid approximation scheme for the one-dimensional Vlasov-Poisson system, that is relevant for arbitrarily long time intervals. For low regularity solutions, we establish an error estimate in the Wasserstein-1 distance, obtaining a convergence rate of order $O(h^{\frac{2}{3}})$, where h denotes the velocity grid size. This establishes the consistency, as the grid size tends to zero, between the pressureless Euler-Poisson and the Vlasov-Poisson systems. Numerical illustrations are given to illustrate the efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [112] [Inverse problems for the diffusion equation with one time observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18814)
*Michel Cristofol,Alexandre Kawano*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究从单一时刻解剖面反演n维热方程位势项的反问题，利用Carleman估计，在已知任意子域上位势的条件下，通过稳定性不等式证明位势项可由固定时刻的测量唯一确定，并讨论此类观测的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热方程位势项反演通常需要多时刻或边界测量，本文旨在探究仅用单一固定时刻的解剖面能否唯一确定位势项，并给出定量稳定性刻画及观测方式的极限。

Method: 基于Carleman估计建立稳定性不等式；在任意子域位势已知的前提下，利用固定时刻的解测量进行唯一性分析。

Result: 证明位势项可由固定时刻的解测量唯一确定，并给出相应的稳定性不等式；同时讨论了该观测方式的最优性。

Conclusion: 固定时刻的单次观测结合局部先验知识足以唯一反演位势项，且Carleman估计是实现该结果的关键工具。

Abstract: We examine the inverse problem of recovering the potential term of an n-dimensional heat equation from a single time solution profile. We show that it is possible to uniquely determine this potential term via a stability inequality using solution measurements taken at a fixed time, assuming the potential is known within an arbitrary subdomain. The approach is based on the Carleman estimate. Additionally, we discuss the optimality of this type of observation.

</details>


### [113] [Local behavior for solutions to inhomogeneous singular parabolic $p$-Laplace equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18815)
*Xia Hao,Yan Li,Zhiwen Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究非齐次奇异抛物p-Laplace方程的Hölder正则性。通过时间重标度与L∞比较估计，证明固定正时刻后的时间Lipschitz正则性，将问题约化为本质上对时间一致的空间水平集测度衰减估计，从而克服内蕴标度法中的经典困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用内蕴标度法证明p-Laplace型拟线性奇异抛物方程解的Hölder正则性，其主要困难在于建立水平集时空测度的衰减估计。本文旨在克服这一障碍。

Method: 考虑带非负、时间无关强迫项及Dirichlet数据的非齐次奇异抛物p-Laplace方程。结合时间重标度论证与L∞比较估计，导出任意固定正经过时间之后的时间Lipschitz正则性，进而将原问题约化为空间水平集测度在时间上本质一致的椭圆型衰减估计。

Result: 获得了固定正经过时间后的时间Lipschitz正则性，并将时空水平集测度衰减问题约化为时间上本质一致的空间测度椭圆型衰减估计。

Conclusion: 该约化使上述水平集测度衰减估计的障碍得以解决，为奇异抛物p-Laplace方程的Hölder正则性证明提供了有效途径。

Abstract: It is known that a major difficulty in proving Hölder regularity for solutions to quasilinear singular parabolic equations of $p$-Laplace type via the method of intrinsic scaling is to establish the decay estimate of the space-time measure of level sets. In this paper, we consider an inhomogeneous singular parabolic $p$-Laplace equation with nonnegative time-independent forcing and Dirichlet data. By combining a time-rescaling argument with $L^\infty$ comparison estimates, we derive Lipschitz regularity in time after any fixed positive elapsed time, which reduces the problem to an elliptic-type decay estimate for the spatial measure of level sets that is essentially uniform in time. This reduction enables us to address the above obstacle.

</details>


### [114] [Isolated singularities of the capillary equation with negative gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18832)
*Bin Deng,Jiahuan Li,Yilu Liu,Xi-nan Ma*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在无对称性、无符号、无单侧有界性及无爆破速率等先验假设下，对负重力毛细方程在穿孔球域内孤立奇点进行分类：解要么在孔点附近有界，要么一致趋于正负无穷。有界情形下解可延拓为局部W^{1,1}分布解，且当2≤n≤7时具有唯一连续代表元；无界情形下解具有显式渐近展开，且高阶水平集为环绕孔点的严格凸连通光滑超曲面，其压力重整图以重数一收敛于圆柱面。还构造了无穷维非径向解族，表明完全渐近展开不能确定奇异解芽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究毛细方程（负重力）在穿孔域中的孤立奇点，旨在去除以往工作中对解的对称性、符号、单侧有界性和爆破速率等先验限制，给出完整的奇点分类与精细渐近结构，并澄清渐近展开对解芽的决定能力。

Method: 结合临界尾部估计与对数BV紧性，配合水平集刚性与毛细方程的平移恒等式进行分类；在临界二维情形引入额外的有限高度分析以排除平移缺陷。

Result: 解在孔点附近要么有界、要么一致趋于±∞；有界情形延拓为W^{1,1}_{loc}分布解，2≤n≤7时连续代表元唯一；无界情形有均匀渐近u(x)=ε((n-1)/|x|-(n+3)/(2(n-1)^2)|x|^3)+O(|x|^5)，高阶水平集为连接光滑严格凸超曲面，压力重整图光滑收敛于∂B_{n-1}×R（重数一）；存在无穷维非径向解族与径向极点各阶代数一致，故完全渐近展开不能确定奇异解芽。

Conclusion: 建立了负重力毛细方程孤立奇点的完整分类：有界解可延拓并在2≤n≤7有唯一连续代表元，无界解具有显式渐近展开及刚性水平集结构；渐近展开不足以判定解芽，非径向解族构成无穷维自由度。

Abstract: We classify isolated singularities of classical solutions to the capillary equation with negative gravity, \[
  \operatorname{div}\frac{Du}{\sqrt{1+|Du|^2}}=-u
  \qquad\text{in }B_R\setminus\{0\}\subset\mathbb R^n,
  \qquad n\ge2, \] without a priori assumptions on symmetry, sign, one-sided boundedness, or blow-up rate. Every such solution is either bounded near the puncture or tends uniformly to $+\infty$ or $-\infty$. In the bounded case, the solution extends across the puncture as a $W^{1,1}_{\mathrm{loc}}$ distributional solution; this extension has a unique continuous representative when $2\le n\le7$. In the unbounded case, for some $\varepsilon\in\{-1,1\}$, \[
  u(x)=\varepsilon\left(\frac{n-1}{|x|}
  -\frac{n+3}{2(n-1)^2}|x|^3\right)+O(|x|^5), \] uniformly in the angular variable. In every fixed smaller ball, all sufficiently high level sets of $\varepsilon u$ are connected, smooth, strictly convex hypersurfaces enclosing the puncture. The corresponding pressure-rescaled graphs converge smoothly with multiplicity one to the round cylinder $\partial B_{n-1}\times\mathbb R$. Nonradial examples form infinite-dimensional families that agree with the radial pole to every algebraic order, so the complete asymptotic expansion does not determine the singular solution germ. The proof of the classification combines critical tail estimates and logarithmic $BV$ compactness with level-set rigidity and the translation identities of the capillary equation. The critical two-dimensional case requires an additional finite-height analysis to exclude a translation defect.

</details>


### [115] [The Regularity datum on time-varying graph domains and Dirichlet--Regularity duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18874)
*Martin Dindoš*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究抛物正则性（Regularity）问题中边界空间 $\dot L^p_{1,1/2}(\partial\Omega)$ 的定义是否依赖于图的参数化，证明一般情况下会不一致，并在 Lewis–Murray 条件下证明其良定义性，进而推广抛物 Dirichlet 与正则性问题的对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时变 Lipschitz 区域上，边界空间通过将数据拉回到平坦空间（范数 $\|\nabla f\|_{L^p}+\|D^{1/2}_t f\|_{L^p}$）来定义。由于同一区域存在多种图参数化，可能产生不同的 $\dot L^p_{1,1/2}(\partial\Omega)$ 定义；该问题仅在时变区域出现，故此前文献未予注意。

Method: 通过构造反例说明不同图参数化下的边界空间确实可以不一致；随后引入额外假设 $D^{1/2}_t\varphi\in\mathrm{BMOpar}$（Lewis–Murray 条件），证明该条件下不同图定义的边界空间一致。

Result: 给出边界空间不一致的反例；证明 Lewis–Murray 条件可完全消除该歧义，使 $\dot L^p_{1,1/2}(\partial\Omega)$ 良定义；并将作者与 E. Nyström 在柱体上建立的抛物 Dirichlet 与 Regularity 问题对偶性推广到 Lewis–Murray 图区域。

Conclusion: 在 Lewis–Murray 图区域上，正则性数据空间是良定义的，且抛物 Dirichlet 与 Regularity 问题的对偶性成立。

Abstract: In this paper we address a basic question that has not been considered in the literature on the parabolic Regularity problem. Let $Ω$ be the region above the graph of a $\mathrm{Lip}(1,\tfrac12)$ function $φ$ on $\mathbb R^{n-1}\times\mathbb R$. The corresponding $\dot L^p_{1,1/2}$ Regularity boundary space on $\partialΩ$ is defined by pulling the datum back to the flat space where we have the norm $\|\nabla f\|_{L^p}+\|D^{1/2}_t f\|_{L^p}$. As there is more than one graph parametrisation of such a domain, this could petentially lead to different notions of $\dot L^p_{1,1/2}(\partialΩ)$. This issue arises only on time-varying domains, which is likely why the issue has not been noticed before.
  We show by example that the boundary spaces can indeed disagree. We then prove that an additional assumption $D^{1/2}_tφ\in\mathrm{BMOpar}$ (called the Lewis--Murray condition), resolves the issue completely: the spaces defined through different graphs agree.\medskip
  This gives us a well-defined Regularity datum space on every Lewis--Murray graph domain. We then extend the duality between the parabolic Dirichlet and Regularity problems, proved on cylinders by the author and E.~Nyström, to such domains.

</details>


### [116] [Existence of Admissible Subsolutions to the Dirichlet Problem for Symmetric Augmented $k$-Hessian Type Equations in Bounded Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18889)
*Quang Hong Dinh,Bang Van Tran,Ngoan Tien Ha,Tho Huu Nguyen,Tien Trong Phan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了对称增广k-Hessian型方程Dirichlet问题容许下解的存在性，核心条件是区域Ω的一致(k-1)-A-凸性；在一定结构条件下该条件也是边界邻域内容许下解存在的必要条件，将Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck关于A≡0的经典结果推广到A≠0的一般情形，结论对增广商Hessian型方程同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck结果仅处理A≡0的k-Hessian型方程，而增广矩阵A(x,z,p)引入后，区域凸性条件需相应推广。作者希望建立一般A≠0情形下容许下解存在的充分且（在结构条件下）必要条件，把一致(k-1)-A-凸性这一由Jiang-Trudinger-Yang提出的概念用于Dirichlet问题的可解性分析。

Method: 针对对称增广k-Hessian型方程的Dirichlet问题，采用Jiang-Trudinger-Yang引入的一致(k-1)-A-凸性作为一个特例条件。对矩阵A(x,z,p)施加结构条件，包括关于z、p的增长性，以及其部分一阶、二阶导数在边界某充分小邻域内足够小。在此框架下证明容许下解的存在性；并通过结构条件分析，证明该凸性条件在边界邻域内也是容许下解存在的必要条件。方法同样适用于增广商Hessian型方程。

Result: 证明了两点：（1）在A的结构条件与Ω的一致(k-1)-A-凸性下，Dirichlet问题存在容许下解，即凸性为充分条件；（2）在A的某些结构条件下，Ω的一致(k-1)-A-凸性亦是边界邻域内容许下解存在的必要条件。由此将A≡0的经典结果完整推广到A≠0，并覆盖增广商Hessian型方程。

Conclusion: 一致(k-1)-A-凸性是可解性（容许下解存在）的核心几何条件，在A≠0的一般增广k-Hessian型方程中兼具充分性与（局部）必要性。该工作推广了Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck的经典定理，并自然延伸到增广商Hessian型方程，为后续可解性与正则性研究提供了框架。

Abstract: We prove the existence of admissible subsolutions to the Dirichlet problem for symmetric augmented $k$-Hessian type equations. An important sufficient condition is the uniform $(k-1)$-$A$-convexity of the domain $Ω,$ where $A(x, z, p)$ is the augmented symmetric matrix appearing in the equation. This condition was originally introduced by F. Jiang, N. S. Trudinger, and X.-P. Yang and we have chosen a special their case. The structural conditions on the matrix $A(x, z, p)$ include its growth with respect to the variables $z$ and $p,$ particularly requiring that some of its first and second derivatives are sufficiently small in a sufficiently small neighborhood of the boundary. Under certain structural conditions on $A(x, z, p),$ the uniform $(k-1)$-$A$-convexity of $Ω$ is also a necessary condition for the existence of admissible subsolutions of the equation in a neighborhood of the boundary. Our results extend the classic result by L. Caffarelli, L. Nirenberg, and J. Spruck from the case $A \equiv 0$ to the general case $A \neq 0.$ Our same theorems are valid also for augmented quotient Hessian type equations.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Lyapunov Operators for Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18894)
*Amartya Mukherjee,Maxwell Fitzsimmons,David C. Del Rey Fernández,Jun Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究将向量场映射为Lyapunov函数的Lyapunov解算子，证明其在吸引域紧子集上适定、唯一且连续，并用FNO实现数据驱动近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造非线性系统Lyapunov函数是稳定性分析的核心难题。传统方法将Lyapunov函数刻画为一阶PDE的解，但通常只针对单一系统求解，难以在不同系统间复用，因此需要研究可跨系统共享的解算子。

Method: 定义基于耗散的Lyapunov PDE对应的Lyapunov解算子，该算子将向量场映射到相应Lyapunov函数。在指数稳定性假设下，于吸引域的紧子集上分析该算子的适定性、唯一性及对向量场与耗散函数扰动的连续性。随后采用Fourier神经算子（FNO）作为该解算子的数据驱动近似。

Result: 理论上证明了该解算子在吸引域紧子集上适定、唯一，且关于向量场和耗散函数的扰动连续。数值实验表明，单个训练好的算子能够准确近似参数化动力学族中的数值Lyapunov函数。

Conclusion: 研究为在一族非线性系统上一致逼近Lyapunov函数提供了理论基础，并展示了神经算子在Lyapunov函数近似方面的潜力。

Abstract: Constructing Lyapunov functions for nonlinear dynamical systems is a central problem in stability analysis, yet remains challenging. Lyapunov functions are commonly characterized as solutions to first-order partial differential equations (PDEs), but these solutions are typically obtained for single systems, limiting their reuse across systems. In this paper, we study the Lyapunov solution operator that maps a vector field to the corresponding Lyapunov function defined by a dissipation-based Lyapunov PDE. We establish that, on compact subsets of the domain of attraction and under exponential stability assumptions, this operator is well-defined, unique, and continuous with respect to perturbations of both the vector field and the dissipation function. These results provide a theoretical foundation for approximating Lyapunov functions uniformly over families of nonlinear systems. Building on these theoretical foundations, we employ Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) as a data-driven approximation of the Lyapunov solution operator. Numerical experiments demonstrate that a single trained operator can accurately approximate the numerical Lyapunov functions across parameterized families of dynamics. This illustrates the potential of neural operators for approximating Lyapunov functions.

</details>


### [118] [Non-rotationally invariant generalised geodesics in the disc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19008)
*Maja Gwozdz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在单位圆盘内研究Brenier松弛最小作用量问题，临界时间T=π、端点对为恒等映射与其负映射，证明临界能量壳层S³上存在平稳作用量最小的广义不可压流，且这些流不具备物理旋转不变性，从而回答了Bernot–Figalli–Santambrogio提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernot、Figalli与Santambrogio曾提出疑问：在临界情形下是否存在不具旋转不变性的作用量最小流。本文旨在构造此类反例，明确临界能量壳层上的最小流不必保持物理旋转对称性，从而回答该开放问题。

Method: 方法基于S³上的归一化曲面测度σ及Hopf商Π=(N,M,L):S³→S²_{1/2}，其中N、M、L由x、v分量二次组合给出，该商为谐振子流的第一积分。取τ=Π_#σ，令g为S²_{1/2}上有界Borel函数，其L^∞(τ)类在(n,m,ℓ)↦(n,m,−ℓ)下为奇函数，δ∈R，且满足1+δg∘Π≥0 σ-a.e.，构造测度dμ_{δ,g}=π(1+δg∘Π)dσ。

Result: 证明dμ_{δ,g}具有Lebesgue空间边缘且是平稳的，从而诱导的路径测度为最小作用量流；并精确刻画该倾斜族中哪些成员具有旋转不变性：当δ≠0时，旋转不变等价于g的L^∞(τ)类在模τ零集意义下轴对称。特别地，取g(n,m,ℓ)=ℓm、0<|δ|<8，可得到严格正的非旋转不变最小流。

Conclusion: 临界能量壳层S³上确实存在非旋转不变的平稳作用量最小广义不可压流，Bernot–Figalli–Santambrogio的问题得到肯定回答；旋转不变性在该倾斜族中可由g的轴对称性精确判定。

Abstract: We study Brenier's relaxed least-action problem in the unit disc $D:=\{x\in\mathbb{R}^2:\ |x|<1\}$ at the critical time $T=π$, with the endpoint pair $i_D$ (the identity on $D$) and $-i_D$. We prove that the critical energy shell \[ S^3=\{(x,v)\in\mathbb{R}^2\times\mathbb{R}^2: |x|^2+|v|^2=1\} \] supports stationary action-minimising generalised incompressible flows that are not invariant under physical rotations. This answers the question posed by Bernot, Figalli, and Santambrogio. Our construction relies on the normalised surface measure $σ$ on $S^3$ and the Hopf quotient \[ Π=(N,M,L):S^3\to\mathbb S^2_{1/2}, \qquad \mathbb S^2_{1/2}:=\{(n,m,\ell)\in\mathbb{R}^3: n^2+m^2+\ell^2=1/4\}. \] Here, \[ N=\frac12(x_1^2+v_1^2-x_2^2-v_2^2),\quad M=x_1x_2+v_1v_2,\quad L=x_1v_2-x_2v_1 . \] This quotient is a first integral of the harmonic-oscillator flow. Let $τ:=Π_\#σ$, let $g$ be a bounded Borel function on $\mathbb S^2_{1/2}$ whose $L^\infty(τ)$-class is odd under $(n,m,\ell)\mapsto(n,m,-\ell)$, and let $δ\in\mathbb{R}$. If $1+δg\circΠ\ge0$ $σ$-a.e., then \[ dμ_{δ,g}=π(1+δg\circΠ)\,dσ\] has Lebesgue spatial marginal and is stationary. The induced path measure is then minimising. We also determine exactly which members of this tilted family are rotationally invariant. If $δ\ne0$, this is equivalent to axisymmetry of the $L^\infty(τ)$-class of $g$, modulo $τ$-null sets. In particular, taking $g(n,m,\ell)=\ell m$ with $0<|δ|<8$ gives strictly positive non-rotationally invariant minimisers.

</details>
