### [119] [Uniform High-Frequency Localization on Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17623)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致度量图上拉普拉斯算子的高频局域化，通过本征函数在观测集上的最小L^2质量定义局域化常数。作者将渐近局域化常数刻画为可达边强度集上线性泛函的最小值，并证明该集合在一般标准Kirchhoff情形下由secular流形的正则Gauss像支配。作者构造了奇异补全锥并证明其包含树、单环及二环闭合图的正则secular边能向量，但构造出一个带两Dirichlet叶的二环图使该原理失效，得到真实常数与补全常数满足C_∞<1/2<C_sing。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子图上高频本征函数的局域化几何是谱几何与量子混沌的核心问题。观测集上的最小L^2质量给出了量化的局域化度量。作者试图确定该高频变分问题能否由secular流形的奇异补全（边界相容奇异扇区）定量刻画，并检验这一补全原理的适用边界。

Method: 将渐近局域化常数化为可达边强度集上线性泛函的最小值；在标准Kirchhoff情形用secular流形的正则Gauss像描述该集；引入边界感知的奇异补全锥并检验其在树、单环图及环秩为2的闭合图中的包含性；构造环秩为2且带两Dirichlet叶的反例图。用精确有理分离子结合经验证的Krawczyk包围盒，得到位于所有边界相容奇异扇区之外的奇异标量secular态，再通过紧轨道闭包上的正径向导数恒等式与递推将其局部分离转化为固定度量图上的精确高频本征序列。

Result: 原始环与外部到外部路径决定正性阈值，但一般不决定正的数值。奇异补全锥包含树、单环图及环秩为2的闭合图的所有正则secular边能向量，但在所构造的带两Dirichlet叶的二环图上失败。对合适的可测观测集ω，真实高频局域化常数C_∞(ω;ℓ)与奇异补全对应常数C_sing(ω;ℓ)满足C_∞<1/2<C_sing。

Conclusion: 奇异补全在若干低复杂度图类中能定量刻画局域化几何，但一般而言不能决定固定量子图上的高频变分问题；其失效由带Dirichlet叶的二环图反例证明。

Abstract: We study uniform high-frequency localization for the Laplacian on compact metric graphs through the least $L^2$-mass that eigenfunctions must place in a prescribed measurable observation set. We first identify this asymptotic localization constant with the minimum of a linear functional over the attainable edge-intensity set; in the generic standard-Kirchhoff setting, this set is governed by the regular Gauss image of the secular manifold. Primitive cycles and exterior-to-exterior paths consequently determine the positivity threshold, but not, in general, the positive numerical value. We introduce a boundary-aware singular-completion cone and prove that it contains all regular secular edge-energy vectors for trees, unicyclic graphs, and closed graphs of cycle rank two. We then construct a cycle-rank-two graph with two Dirichlet leaves for which this completion principle fails. An exact rational separator, combined with a validated Krawczyk enclosure, yields a nonsingular scalar secular state lying outside every boundary-compatible singular sector. A positive radial derivative identity and recurrence in the compact orbit closure convert this local separation into an exact high-frequency eigensequence for a single fixed metric. For a suitable measurable observation set, the true high-frequency localization constant $C_\infty(ω;\ell)$ and its singular-completion counterpart $C_{\mathrm{sing}}(ω;\ell)$ satisfy $$C_\infty(ω;\ell)<1/2<C_{\mathrm{sing}}(ω;\ell)$$. Thus, singular completion captures the quantitative localization geometry in several low-complexity classes but does not, in general, determine the high-frequency variational problem on a fixed quantum graph.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [A $\sqrt{2}$-Approximation to the Bilu-Linial Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17551)
*Zejun Huang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究正则图的边签名问题，通过构造辅助二部图并应用二部图签名论证，证明最大度为d的有限简单图存在边签名σ使得ρ(A_σ)≤4√(⌈d/2⌉-1)≤2√(2(d-1))，即在Ramanujan界的至多√2倍因子范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bilu和Linial猜想每个d-正则图（d≥2）存在边签名使签名邻接矩阵谱半径满足Ramanujan界2√(d-1)，并证明了O(√(d log^3 d))的上界。Marcus、Spielman和Srivastava用交错多项式方法证实了猜想的一个方向（最大特征值≤2√(d-1)）。本文旨在进一步逼近完整的Ramanujan谱半径界。

Method: 从图G的平衡定向构造一个辅助二部图，并应用二部图签名论证（bipartite signing argument）进行分析。

Result: 证明每个最大度d≥3的有限简单图G都存在边签名σ，使得谱半径ρ(A_σ)≤4√(⌈d/2⌉-1)≤2√(2(d-1))，即所得界至多为猜想Ramanujan界的√2倍。

Conclusion: 论文将Ramanujan谱半径猜想的界推进到至多相差√2因子的范围，显著改进了先前O(√(d log^3 d))的界，且适用于更一般的最大度为d的简单图而非仅d-正则图。

Abstract: Bilu and Linial conjectured that if $d\ge 2$, then every $d$-regular graph $G$ has an edge signing $σ:E(G)\to\{-1,1\}$ such that its signed adjacency matrix $A_σ$ satisfies the Ramanujan bound \begin{equation*}
  ρ(A_σ)\le 2\sqrt{d-1} \end{equation*} and they proved that \begin{equation*}
  ρ(A_σ)=O\!\left(\sqrt{d\log^3 d}\right). \end{equation*}
  Using a method of interlacing polynomials, Marcus, Spielman, and Srivastava confirmed one side of this conjecture that there is a signing $σ$ for which
  \[
  λ_{\max}(A_σ)\le 2\sqrt{d-1}. \] By constructing an auxiliary bipartite graph from a balanced orientation of $G$ and applying a bipartite signing argument, we prove that every finite simple graph $G$ of maximum degree $d\ge 3$ admits an edge signing $σ$ such that \[
  ρ(A_σ)
  \le 4\sqrt{\left\lceil \frac d2\right\rceil-1}
  \le 2\sqrt{2(d-1)}. \] Thus we obtain a bound within a factor at most $\sqrt2$ of the conjectured Ramanujan bound.

</details>


### [121] [An improved lower bound for the blowup defective chromatic separation constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17557)
*Guillaume Lecomte*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文改进Guo-Kang-Zwaneveld猜想的反例下界：给出一个40顶点图W，使χ(W)/χ²(W⊠K₃)≥11/10，从而C*≥11/10>30/29，且d=2即为可能的最小缺陷值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guo、Kang与Zwaneveld提出猜想χ(G)=χ^d(G⊠K_{d+1})。Norin与Steiner否定该猜想，构造出无穷多个d满足χ(G)≥(30/29)χ^d(G⊠K_{d+1})的图，并证明普遍上界χ(G)≤2χ^d(G⊠K_{d+1})。由此C*=sup_d C_d落在[30/29,2]区间。本文旨在改进该下界，并探究分离能在多小的缺陷d处首次出现。

Method: 构造一个显式的40顶点图W，满足χ(W)=11且χ²(W⊠K₃)=10；所有参数均通过证明确立。唯一依赖计算机辅助的输入是某个30顶点、10色的列表着色实例(B,L)的不可列表着色性，该事实由可独立检验的DRAT反驳证书予以验证。

Result: 得到C*≥C₂≥11/10>30/29，即在最小可能缺陷d=2处（d<2时不可能产生此类分离）即已实现分离，改进了此前30/29的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作将C*的下界由30/29提升至11/10，证明分离在最小缺陷d=2时即发生，除一处经DRAT证书认证的计算机辅助列表着色结果外，其余参数均为严格数学证明。

Abstract: For a graph $G$ and an integer $d \geq 0$, let $χ^d(G)$ denote the $d$-defective chromatic number, and let $G \boxtimes K_{d+1}$ be the $(d+1)$-fold clique blowup of $G$. Norin and Steiner disproved the conjecture $χ(G) = χ^d(G \boxtimes K_{d+1})$ of Guo, Kang and Zwaneveld by exhibiting, for infinitely many $d$, graphs with $χ(G) \geq (30/29) χ^d(G \boxtimes K_{d+1})$, and they proved the universal upper bound $χ(G) \leq 2 χ^d(G \boxtimes K_{d+1})$. Writing $C_d = \sup_G χ(G)/χ^d(G \boxtimes K_{d+1})$ and $C^* = \sup_d C_d$, their results give $C^* \in [30/29, 2]$. We improve the lower bound: we exhibit an explicit 40-vertex graph $W$ with $χ(W) = 11$ and $χ^2(W \boxtimes K_3) = 10$, so that $C^* \geq C_2 \geq 11/10 > 30/29$, already at the smallest defect for which such a separation is possible, namely $d = 2$. All parameters are established by the proofs; the only computer-assisted input, the non-list-colourability of a certain 30-vertex, 10-colour list instance $(B,L)$, is certified by an independently checkable DRAT refutation.

</details>


### [122] [A closed form for the block-statistic generating function of the $2\times n$ rectangle: the two-row case of Kahane's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17561)
*Patrick White*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Kahane关于斜形状细胞偏序集上一个t-统计量恒等式的猜想在2×n矩形这一首个二维情形下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kahane证明了栅栏偏序集上的恒等式n!Ω(P;t)=Σ_σ t^{bl(σ)}，并猜想该恒等式可推广到任意斜形状的细胞偏序集（用构造性定义的bl̃替换bl）。2×n矩形是首个真正意义上的二维情形，此时块不再是路径区间，统计量同时依赖两行，具有本质困难，因此成为验证该猜想的关键一步。

Method: 引入双参数增长状态生成函数W_n(α,β;t)，按底行最小元的秩α与最右顶胞块根的秩β对Σ_σ t^{bl̃(σ)}进行加细，证明闭式W_n(α,β;t)=(α-1)!·B(n,β-2)·S_n(α,β;t)，其中B(n,k)为Catalan三角形、S_n为上升阶乘骨架。证明通过Kahane的列插入转移对n作归纳，其关键结构事实是新插入顶胞下方的块根恒为全局底行最小元，从而把归纳归约为Catalan三角形的计算。

Result: 对(α,β)求和后望远镜化为C_n·t^{n̅}(t+1)^{n̅}，再由MacMahon乘积公式等于(2n)!·Ω(R_{2,n};t)，即猜想在2×n矩形上成立。所有关键步骤均为一般n的完整论证；转移表与归纳步通过独立从零实现的穷举枚举（至n=8与n=9）交叉验证。

Conclusion: 作者证明了Kahane猜想在2×n矩形这一首个二维情形下成立，给出双参数生成函数的闭式及其Catalan三角形归约。一般斜形状与m>2行的情形仍未解决。

Abstract: Kahane (arXiv:2607.11225) proves that for a fence poset $P$ of size $n$, $n!\,Ω(P;t)=\sum_{σ\in S_n} t^{\mathrm{bl}(σ)}$, with $\mathrm{bl}(σ)$ a canonical block count, and conjectures (Conjecture 4.4) that the identity extends to the cell poset of any skew shape, with $\mathrm{bl}$ replaced by a constructively-defined extension $\widetilde{\mathrm{bl}}$. We prove the conjecture for every $2\times n$ rectangle -- the first genuinely two-dimensional case, where blocks are no longer path-intervals and the statistic is a correlated function of both rows. We introduce a two-parameter growth-state generating function $W_n(α,β;t)$ refining $\sum_σt^{\widetilde{\mathrm{bl}}(σ)}$ by the rank $α$ of the bottom-row minimum and the rank $β$ of the rightmost top cell's block-root, and prove the closed form $W_n(α,β;t)=(α-1)!\,B(n,β-2)\,S_n(α,β;t)$ ($n\ge 1$), where $B(n,k)$ is the Catalan triangle and $S_n$ a rising-factorial skeleton. The proof is an induction on $n$ through Kahane's column-insertion transition, whose load-bearing structural fact -- the block-root below a newly inserted top cell is always the global bottom-row minimum -- reduces the induction to a Catalan-triangle computation. Summing over $(α,β)$ telescopes to $C_n\,t^{\overline{n}}(t+1)^{\overline{n}}$, which by MacMahon's product formula equals $(2n)!\,Ω(R_{2,n};t)$. Every load-bearing step is a complete general-$n$ argument; the transition table and the inductive step are cross-checked by exhaustive enumeration (through $n=8$ and $n=9$) via an independent from-scratch implementation. General skew shapes and $m>2$ rows remain open.

</details>


### [123] [Exact three-component covers in 2-coloured random bipartite graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17567)
*Xiao-Chuan Liu,Xu Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了随机二部图 $G(n,n,p)$（$p\gg\sqrt{\log n/n}$）中 Fernández–Pavez-Signé–Stein 双色三分量猜想的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Fernández、Pavez-Signé 与 Stein 提出的双色三分量猜想在随机二部图上的情形，即考察图的双色边染色能否被少数单色连通分量覆盖。

Method: 基于关于公共邻域并集的一致膨胀引理，并结合一种交替公共邻域膨胀论证。

Result: 证明若 $G\sim G(n,n,p)$ 且 $p\gg\sqrt{\log n/n}$，则以高概率 $G$ 的任意红蓝边染色都存在至多三个单色连通分量覆盖其顶点集。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了随机二部图中的双色三分量猜想，验证了稀疏阈值以上的覆盖性质。

Abstract: We resolve the two-colour three-component conjecture of Fernández, Pavez-Signé and Stein for random bipartite graphs. More precisely, we prove that if $G\sim G(n,n,p)$ and $p\gg\sqrt{\log n/n}$, then with high probability every red--blue edge-colouring of $G$ admits a cover of its vertex set by at most three monochromatic connected components. The proof is based on a uniform expansion lemma for unions of common neighbourhoods and an alternating common-neighbourhood expansion argument.

</details>


### [124] [Integer Realization of an Equivelar Octahedron of Genus 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17700)
*Ruslan Mizhaev*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了一个亏格3多面体曲面的整数坐标实现，由8个平面正九边形面构成，含24个顶点、36条边，每顶点关联3个面，任意两面共享一条边（20对共享1条，8对共享2条）。文中给出了顶点坐标、面行走序列与平面方程，并通过精确验证确认所有面共面、曲面闭合可定向、无意外自交，且具有C₄对称性；该构造基于早期构造并保持其关联结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亏格大于0的多面体曲面（尤其是有限面数、等边/正多边形面的实现）在几何与组合构造中较难显式给出；已有构造可能缺乏整数坐标或严格精确验证。本文旨在给出一个具有整数坐标、面结构规整（8个平面正九边形）的亏格3曲面显式实现，并保持既有构造的关联结构。

Method: 采用整数坐标构造一个亏格3的多面体曲面：定义24个顶点、36条边与8个平面正九边形面，使每顶点关联3个面；给出顶点坐标、面行走与平面方程，并通过精确（符号/有理数）计算进行验证。构造方法基于此前的构造，并保持其组合关联结构，同时检查平面性、闭合性、可定向性与是否出现非预期相交，并识别对称性。

Result: 得到一个整数坐标的亏格3多面体曲面实现：8个平面正九边形面、24顶点、36边、3-valent；任意两面均共享边，其中20对面共享1条边、8对面共享2条边。精确验证表明：所有面共面、曲面闭合且可定向、无非预期相交，并具有C₄对称性。

Conclusion: 作者成功构造并严格验证了一个具有C₄对称性的整数坐标亏格3多面体曲面，其面为8个平面正九边形，保持了原有构造的关联结构。该显式实现为亏格3曲面的几何构造与验证提供了具体实例，并强调精确计算在确认平面性、闭合性、可定向性与无自交中的关键作用。

Abstract: We present an integer-coordinate realization of a genus-3 polyhedral surface with eight planar simple nonagonal faces. It has 24 vertices and 36 edges, and each vertex is incident with three faces. Every pair of faces shares an edge: 20 pairs share one edge and 8 pairs share two. Vertex coordinates, face walks, and plane equations are given. Exact verification confirms that all faces are planar, the surface is closed and orientable, no unintended intersections occur, and the realization has $C_4$ symmetry. The construction is based on an earlier construction and preserves its incidence structure.

</details>


### [125] [The Theta Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17744)
*Giovanni Interdonato,Alessandro Iraci*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在有标记升与可缩谷的有标Dyck路径上定义对角反转统计量，给出Theta猜想的一个显式二元细化候选，并证明其Schröder情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D'Adderio、Iraci与Vanden Wyngaerd提出的单变量Theta猜想虽已被证明，但缺乏更精细的二元组合细化及其对称函数解释。本文旨在构造该细化的显式候选，并为其提供部分理论支持。

Method: 在有标Dyck路径上同时装饰升（rise）与可缩谷（contractible valley），定义对角反转（diagonal-inversion）统计量；通过令q=1退化回单变量猜想；并利用两个高斯乘积恒等式，以更精细的组合论证计算与e_{n-d}h_d的内积，证明Schröder情形。

Result: 新猜想在任一装饰参数消失时分别退化为Delta猜想的升版本与谷版本；给出对称函数Θ_{e_l}Θ_{e_k}∇e_{n-k-l}的组合解释，并猜测其为具有两组交换变量与两组反交换变量的对角余不变量分次模的Frobenius特征；证明了该猜想的Schröder情形。

Conclusion: 该工作为Theta猜想及其触及细化提供了显式的二元组合模型，并验证了Schröder情形，为完整猜想的证明提供了新工具与证据。

Abstract: We define a diagonal-inversion statistic on labeled Dyck paths carrying both decorated rises and decorated contractible valleys. This gives an explicit candidate for a bivariate refinement of the univariate Theta conjecture of D'Adderio, Iraci, and Vanden Wyngaerd, recently proved by D'Adderio, Pagaria, and the authors of this work. The univariate conjecture can be recovered from our bivariate version by setting $q=1$.
  The new conjecture recovers the rise and valley versions of the Delta conjecture when either decoration parameter vanishes, and provides a combinatorial interpretation of the symmetric function $Θ_{e_l} Θ_{e_k}\nabla e_{n-k-l}$, which is also conjectured to be the Frobenius characteristic of a certain graded module of diagonal coinvariants with two sets of commuting variables and two sets of anticommuting variables.
  In support of the Theta conjecture and its touching refinement, we prove its Schröder case, that is, the scalar product of the symmetric function side with $e_{n-d} h_d$ matches the combinatorial side restricted to Schröder paths. The proof uses a finer combinatorial argument than the previously known cases, leveraging two Gaussian product identities.

</details>


### [126] [Generating Functions and the Minimum Spectral Radius in Strongly Connected Digraphs with $m+2$ Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18367)
*Rostislav Klech*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究含 m 个顶点、m+2 条边的强连通有向图类 $\mathcal{SC}_{m+2}(m)$ 的最小邻接谱半径，借助耳分解给出完整结构分类，将极值问题约化为有限族多项式的比较，证明最小谱半径由 $P_{\min}(z)=1-2z^{m-1}-z^m$ 的最小正根 $R_m$ 决定，即 $\rho_{\min}=R_m^{-1}$，并确定了极值图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定稀疏强连通有向图中邻接谱半径的极值问题，刻画达到最小谱半径的图结构，是谱图理论中的基本问题。已有结果多集中于无向图或特定图类，而对边数接近顶点数的强连通有向图类缺少完整的结构分类与精确的极值结论，因此需要系统研究该类图的最小谱半径。

Method: 1) 利用有向路径的生成函数，为相关有向图关联拓扑多项式，使其最小正根决定对应的谱半径；2) 基于耳分解（ear decomposition），证明 $\mathcal{SC}_{m+2}(m)$ 中每个有向图都可由一个蝴蝶图（butterfly digraph）附加单条耳得到，从而给出该类图的完整结构分类；3) 将极值问题约化为有限个多项式族在其可实现性条件下的优化与比较；4) 对候选多项式族进行比较，确定决定最小值的目标多项式 $P_{\min}(z)=1-2z^{m-1}-z^m$ 及其唯一正根 $R_m\in(0,1)$。

Result: 证明了 $\min_{G\in\mathcal{SC}_{m+2}(m)}\rho(G)=R_m^{-1}$，其中 $R_m$ 是 $P_{\min}(z)=1-2z^{m-1}-z^m$ 的唯一正根。对于 $m\ge4$，最小值在同构意义下唯一地由交叉弦圈（cross-chorded cycle）$\mathcal{C}_m^\times$ 取得；对于 $m=3$，恰有两个不同构的极小图，其谱半径均为黄金比 $(1+\sqrt5)/2$。此外建立了界 $2^{1/(m-1)}<\rho(\mathcal{C}_m^\times)<3^{1/(m-1)}$。

Conclusion: 在强连通有向图类 $\mathcal{SC}_{m+2}(m)$ 中，最小邻接谱半径被完全刻画：其倒数为特定拓扑多项式 $P_{\min}$ 的最小正根，并由交叉弦圈 $\mathcal{C}_m^\times$ 唯一达到（$m\ge4$）；$m=3$ 时有两个极小图。所得上下界进一步给出了极小谱半径的定量估计。

Abstract: We study the minimum adjacency spectral radius in the class $\mathcal{SC}_{m+2}(m)$ of strongly connected digraphs with $m$ vertices and $m+2$ edges. Using generating functions for directed paths, we associate with the relevant digraphs topological polynomials whose smallest positive roots determine the corresponding spectral radii. Based on an ear decomposition, we obtain a complete structural classification of $\mathcal{SC}_{m+2}(m)$ by showing that every digraph in this class can be obtained from a butterfly digraph by attaching a single ear. This reduces the extremal problem to the optimization and comparison of finitely many polynomial families subject to their realizability conditions. We prove that the minimum spectral radius is determined by the polynomial $P_{\min}(z)=1-2z^{m-1}-z^m$. If $R_m\in(0,1)$ denotes its unique root, then $\min_{G\in\mathcal{SC}_{m+2}(m)}ρ(G)=R_m^{-1}$. For $m\geq4$, the minimum is attained, up to isomorphism, uniquely by the cross-chorded cycle $\mathcal{C}_m^\times$. For $m=3$, there are exactly two non-isomorphic minimizers, both with spectral radius $(1+\sqrt5)/2$. Finally, we establish the bounds $2^{1/(m-1)}<ρ\left(\mathcal{C}_m^\times\right)<3^{1/(m-1)}$.

</details>


### [127] [Every graph with no $K_7^=$ minor is 6-colorable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17760)
*Zdeněk Dvořák,Sergey Norin,Neil Rahman*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了不含 $K_7^=$-minor 的图是 6-可着色的，其中 $K_7^=$ 为 $K_7$ 删去两条独立边所得的图，从而在受限情形下推进了 Hadwiger 猜想的首个开放情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hadwiger 猜想的首个开放情形断言所有不含 $K_7$-minor 的图都是 6-可着色的，该问题尚未解决；本文的目标是在一个更受限的图类（不含 $K_7^=$-minor）上取得突破，并希望由此提供可推广的工具。

Method: 核心是一个独立的密度结果：任意 5-连通且不含 $K_7^=$-minor 的图，若顶点数 $n\ge 6$，则边数至多为 $4n-8$。作者基于该密度定理推导出 6-可着色性。

Result: 证明了对 $K_7^=$-minor-free 图 Hadwiger 猜想的首个开放情形成立，即这类图是 6-可着色的；同时建立了上述 $4n-8$ 的边数上界。

Conclusion: 在 $K_7^=$-minor-free 这一子类中解决了 Hadwiger 猜想首个开放情形，所依赖的 5-连通密度上界本身具有独立价值，可能为后续更一般情形的研究提供思路。

Abstract: The first open case of Hadwiger's conjecture states that every $K_7$-minor-free graph is 6-colorable. We prove that this is the case for $K_7^=$-minor-free graphs, where $K_7^=$ denotes the graph obtained from $K_7$ by deleting two independent edges. The proof is based on an independently interesting density result: Every 5-connected $K_7^=$-minor-free graph with $n\ge 6$ vertices has at most $4n-8$ edges.

</details>


### [128] [Generalized short monochromatic odd cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17773)
*Sammy Luo,Zixuan Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文回应Noga Alon的问题，将Janzer与Yip的结论推广：任意$K_{r^k+1}$的$k$边着色中必含一个单色子图，其色数至少为$r+1$且直径为$O(k^{3/2}r^{k/2}\log r)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作由Noga Alon提出的问题驱动，旨在推广Janzer与Yip关于单色子图（高色数且小直径）的已有结果。

Method: 通过对完全图$K_{r^k+1}$的任意$k$边着色进行分析，证明其中存在同时满足色数与直径约束的单色子图。

Result: 证明了每个$k$边着色的$K_{r^k+1}$都包含一个单色子图，其色数至少为$r+1$，直径为$O(k^{3/2}r^{k/2}\log r)$。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了Janzer与Yip的定理，并回答了Noga Alon提出的问题。

Abstract: Answering a question of Noga Alon, we generalize a result of Janzer and Yip to show that every $k$-edge-coloring of $K_{r^k+1}$ contains a monochromatic subgraph with chromatic number at least $r+1$ and diameter $O(k^{3/2}r^{k/2}\log r)$.

</details>


### [129] [When are random regular triangle-free graphs bipartite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17958)
*Gregory DeCamillis,Pu Gao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 随机 d-正则无三角图在 d=(√3/2)√(n log n) 处出现尖锐相变：d 小于该阈值时几乎必然非二部，d 大于该阈值时几乎必然二部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画随机 d-正则无三角图的二部性随平均度 d 变化的结构行为，定位其相变临界点。

Method: 研究随机 d-正则无三角图模型（random d-regular triangle-free graphs），通过阈值分析确定二部性发生的临界平均度。

Result: 在 d=(√3/2)√(n log n) 处发生尖锐相变（sharp phase transition）：d 较小时图几乎必然（asymptotically almost surely）非二部；d 较大时图几乎必然二部。

Conclusion: 随机 d-正则无三角图的二部性存在由 d 控制的尖锐阈值，临界值为 d=(√3/2)√(n log n)。

Abstract: We study the structure of random $d$-regular triangle-free graphs and show that a sharp phase transition occurs at $d=\frac{\sqrt 3}{2}\sqrt{n \log n}$. For smaller $d$, asymptotically almost surely the graph is non-bipartite, whereas for greater $d$, asymptotically almost surely the graph is bipartite.

</details>


### [130] [Perfect state transfer under matrix powers: parity and spectral arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18018)
*Xingkun Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文刻画了实对称矩阵幂 \(H^k\) 从顶点 \(a\) 到 \(b\) 具有完美态传输（PST）的指数 \(k\)：若支撑特征值为同一正数的整数倍，则奇指数等价于 \(H\)，正偶指数等价于 \(H^2\)，并用支撑谱差的 gcd 确定最小传输时间；对有理对称矩阵及若干图类给出完整分类，特别地 \(P_7\) 的邻接矩阵仅在 \(k=2\) 时从顶点 2 到 6 有 PST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完美态传输通常研究单次演化 \(e^{-iHt}\)，本文关注矩阵幂 \(H^k\) 情形下的 PST，旨在刻画哪些指数 \(k\) 能实现 PST，并给出最小传输时间与图类分类，统一处理整数倍谱、有理矩阵、二部图及超立方体、环、Johnson 图等情形。

Method: 设 \(H\) 实对称，\(a,b\) 为不同顶点；若支撑特征值均为某正数的整数倍，证明每个奇指数约化为 \(H\)，每个正偶指数约化为 \(H^2\)；利用支撑谱差的最大公约数确定最小传输时间。对有 rational 对称矩阵，由源顶点支撑关于零的对称性推出奇幂等价而无需可公度假设，涵盖所有二部图；若源顶点支撑含零，则一个正偶幂下的 PST 推出所有正偶幂下的 PST。对三点二次谱且外投影符号一致并与中心符号不同者，非零 rational 平移仅留下一个 PST 指数。

Result: 得到 \(H^k\) 的 PST 指数分类与最小传输时间公式；证明有理对称矩阵中源顶点支撑关于零的对称性可给出奇幂等价，包含所有二部图；源顶点支撑含零时正偶幂 PST 具有全偶幂传递性；三点二次谱情形下 rational 平移后仅一个 PST 指数；完整分类超立方体、环、Johnson 图的所有邻接幂，以及路径的所有邻接平方；特别地，\(P_7\) 的邻接矩阵仅在指数 2 时从顶点 2 到顶点 6 有 PST。

Conclusion: 该工作将 PST 从连续时间演化拓展到矩阵幂，建立了基于支撑谱和 gcd 的指数分类框架，并给出多类图的完整结果；其中 \(P_7\) 的邻接矩阵仅在平方幂时具有顶点 2 到 6 的 PST 是代表性结论。

Abstract: For a real symmetric matrix $H$ and distinct vertices $a,b$, we classify exponents $k$ for which $H^k$ has perfect state transfer (PST) from $a$ to $b$. If their supported eigenvalues are integer multiples of a common positive number, every odd exponent reduces to $H$ and every positive even exponent reduces to $H^2$. We determine the minimum transfer times using a greatest common divisor of supported spectral differences. For rational symmetric matrices, symmetry of the source vertex support about zero implies the odd-power equivalence without a commensurability assumption; this includes all bipartite graphs. If the source vertex supports zero, PST under one positive even power implies PST under every positive even power. For a symmetric three-point quadratic spectrum whose outer projection signs agree and differ from the central sign, a nonzero rational shift leaves exactly one PST exponent. We classify all adjacency powers of hypercubes, cycles, and Johnson graphs, and all adjacency squares of paths. In particular, the adjacency matrix of $P_7$ has PST from vertex $2$ to vertex $6$ only at exponent $2$.

</details>


### [131] [Demazure crystals for flagged set-valued reverse plane partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18151)
*Siddheswar Kundu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 在给定斜形状与旗标的带旗集合值反平面分拆集合上构造Demazure晶体结构，统一推广了已知的两类Demazure晶体结构，并据此给出带旗混合Grothendieck多项式的key多项式展开及其与稳定/对偶稳定Grothendieck多项式的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带旗半标准集合值表与带旗反平面分拆此前各自拥有独立的Demazure晶体结构，缺乏统一框架；同时Guo–Kang–Liu引入的带旗混合Grothendieck多项式缺少组合展开与经典Grothendieck多项式之间的显式联系。

Method: 在斜形状λ/μ与旗标Φ所对应的带旗集合值反平面分拆集合上构造Demazure晶体结构，把原有的两类Demazure晶体作为特例纳入统一框架。

Result: 得到带旗混合Grothendieck多项式H_{λ/μ}(x_Φ;t;w)按key多项式的组合展开，并进一步将混合Grothendieck多项式H_{λ/μ}(x;t;w)用稳定Grothendieck多项式G_ν(x)与对偶稳定Grothendieck多项式g_ν(x)表示。

Conclusion: 所构造的Demazure晶体结构为带旗集合值表与带旗反平面分拆提供了统一推广，并由此导出带旗混合Grothendieck多项式的key展开及其与两类稳定Grothendieck多项式的表达式。

Abstract: In this paper, we build a Demazure crystal structure on the set of all flagged set-valued reverse plane partitions of a given skew shape $λ/μ$ and flag $Φ$. Our construction provides a unified generalization of the Demazure crystal structures previously known for the set of all flagged semi-standard set-valued tableaux and the set of all flagged reverse plane partitions. Consequently, we obtain a combinatorial expansion for the flagged hybrid Grothendieck polynomial $H_{λ/μ}(\mathbf{x}_Φ;\mathbf{t};\mathbf{w})$, introduced by Guo--Kang--Liu, in terms of key polynomials. Applying this expansion, we express the hybrid Grothendieck polynomial $H_{λ/μ}(\mathbf{x};\mathbf{t};\mathbf{w})$ in terms of both the stable Grothendieck polynomials $G_ν(\mathbf{x})$ and the dual stable Grothendieck polynomials $g_ν(\mathbf{x})$.

</details>


### [132] [A near-linear upper bound for Burr's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18175)
*Liangdong Fan,Junying Lu,Yaojun Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究有向图中保证包含任意k顶点定向树所需的最小色数阈值f(k)，利用吸收集方法将上界改进为O(k log k)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burr(1980)给出f(k)≤(k−1)^2并猜想f(k)=2k−2；Bessy等人(2025)证明f(k)=O(k^{3/2})。本文旨在进一步缩小该上界与猜想之间的差距。

Method: 采用吸收集方法（absorbing set method）证明f(k)≤⌊31 log(k!)⌋=O(k log k)。

Result: 证明了f(k)≤⌊31 log(k!)⌋，即f(k)=O(k log k)，改进了此前O(k^{3/2})的上界。

Conclusion: 该结果显著推进了对Burr猜想（f(k)=2k−2）的研究，使已知上界从O(k^{3/2})降至O(k log k)。

Abstract: Let $f(k)$ denote the smallest integer such that every oriented graph $D$ with chromatic number at least $f(k)$ contains every oriented tree on $k$ vertices. Burr (1980) showed that $f(k)\le (k-1)^2$ and conjectured that $f(k)=2k-2$. Bessy, Gonçalves and Reinald (2025) proved that $f(k)=O(k^{3/2})$. In this paper, by using an absorbing set method, we show that $f(k)\le \lfloor 31\log (k!)\rfloor=O(k\log k)$.

</details>


### [133] [Turán-type extremal problems for unbalanced signed graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18205)
*Linfeng Xie,Keheng Zhu,Xiaogang Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将经典Turán定理与Nikiforov谱Turán定理推广至符号图，并确定了不含平衡完全符号子图的不平衡符号图中的第二最大谱半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Turán定理及其谱版本是极值图论的核心结果，但针对符号图（signed graphs）的对应推广尚不完善，尤其缺乏对平衡完全符号子图的极值刻画。

Method: 建立符号图的Turán型结果：先将经典Turán定理推广到符号图，再将Nikiforov的谱Turán定理推广到符号图；并针对不含r+1个顶点的平衡完全符号子图的不平衡符号图，通过谱极值分析确定其次大谱半径。

Result: 得到了符号图上经典Turán定理的推广形式与谱Turán定理的推广形式；确定了不含平衡完全符号子图（r+1个顶点）的所有不平衡符号图中第二最大谱半径及其对应极图。

Conclusion: 该工作将Turán型极值理论系统扩展至符号图框架，为符号图谱极值问题提供了新的理论工具与结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish two Turán-type results for signed graphs. We first generalize the classical Turán theorem to signed graphs and then extend Nikiforov's spectral Turán theorem to signed graphs. Moreover, we determine the second maximum spectral radius among all unbalanced signed graphs that contain no balanced complete signed subgraph on \(r+1\) vertices.

</details>


### [134] [Regular dyadic triangulations of delta-matroid polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18331)
*Mathieu Vallée*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究B型广义permutohedron与delta-matroid多胞形的剖分性质，证明它们普遍允许正则二进剖分，并刻画了唯一的非幺模delta-matroid单形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A型整数广义permutohedron（特别是拟阵基多胞形）已被证明具有正则幺模剖分；但B型情形失效，存在归一化体积为2且除顶点外无其他格点的delta-matroid单形，因而无幺模剖分。作者希望刻画这一障碍何时唯一出现，并寻找B型多胞形可普遍满足的较弱剖分性质。

Method: 引入“完全二进系统”这一2-局部全幺模性的类比，证明B型根系构型构成完全二进系统；据此构造正则二进剖分，即格剖分中极大单形的归一化体积均为2的幂。

Result: 证明上述delta-matroid单形在0/1立方体的自然对称及删除常坐标意义下是唯一的非幺模delta-matroid单形；进一步证明每个delta-matroid多胞形及每个整数B型广义permutohedron都容许正则二进剖分。

Conclusion: B型广义permutohedron与delta-matroid多胞形满足整数分解性质的二进版本，且每维中相应指数可统一选取，尽管普通整数分解对delta-matroid多胞形可能失效。

Abstract: Backman and Liu proved that every integral generalized permutohedron of type $A$, and in particular every matroid base polytope, admits a regular unimodular triangulation. The analogous statement fails in type $B$: the delta-matroid simplex \[\operatorname*{conv}\{\mathbf{0},\ e_1+e_2,\ e_1+e_3,\ e_2+e_3\}\] has normalized volume $2$ and no lattice points other than its vertices, so it has no unimodular triangulation. We show moreover that, up to the natural symmetries of the $0/1$ cube and deletion of constant coordinates, it is the unique non-unimodular delta-matroid polytope that is a simplex. We prove instead that every delta-matroid polytope admits a regular dyadic triangulation, meaning a lattice triangulation whose maximal simplices have normalized volumes that are powers of two. More generally, every integral type $B$ generalized permutohedron admits such a triangulation. The main lattice-theoretic ingredient is that the type $B$ root configuration forms a totally dyadic system, a $2$-local analogue of total unimodularity. As a consequence, these polytopes satisfy a dyadic version of the integer decomposition property. In each dimension the corresponding exponent can be chosen uniformly, even though ordinary integer decomposition can fail for delta-matroid polytopes.

</details>


### [135] [A computational framework for multidimensional loop-cocyclic Hadamard matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18380)
*Manuel González-Regadera,Raúl M. Falcón*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将 Hadamard 矩阵的余循环构造从群推广到有限 loop，引入 δ-相容 2-上链对概念，将搜索空间约化为普通 2-余循环空间并消除 2^n 冗余因子，三维构造细化了经典余循环 Hadamard 分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hadamard 矩阵的余循环构造已通过含结合性障碍的上同调理论从群推广到 loop，在 F_2 上 loop-余循环可化为齐次线性系统的解，便于精确计算。本文旨在利用这一框架研究由有限 loop 产生的多维 Hadamard 矩阵，并推广经典余循环构造。

Method: 提出 loop L 上 2-上链的 δ-相容对概念，作为通常 2-余循环恒等式的自然推广，允许使用两个不同的 2-上链。证明其构成的向量空间自然同构于 2-余循环空间与 1-上链空间的直积，从而将多维 Hadamard 矩阵的余循环构造从群推广到任意 loop，并保持计算优势。

Result: 所得分解表明 Hadamard 等价类的判定可从全体 δ-相容对空间约化到较小的普通 2-余循环空间，从搜索空间中消除 2^n 的冗余因子。对 4 阶和 8 阶所有群及一个 8 阶非结合 loop 的计算表明，三维构造细化了经典余循环 Hadamard 分类：二维时所有例子坍缩为单一等价类，三维时分裂为多个不同类，且非结合 loop 产生了一个不来自任何所考虑群的三维 Hadamard 类。

Conclusion: 所提出的三维构造能检测在余循环矩阵层面不可见的结构信息，将余循环 Hadamard 分类真正推广到非结合结构，并验证了多维框架在 loop 上的有效性与计算可行性。

Abstract: The cocyclic development of Hadamard matrices has recently been extended from groups to loops by means of a cohomology theory that incorporates associativity obstructions. Over the finite field $\mathbb F_2$, the resulting loop-cocycles can be computed as solutions of homogeneous linear systems, making the framework suitable for exact computation. In this paper, we investigate multidimensional Hadamard matrices arising from finite loops. We introduce the notion of $δ$-compatible pairs of $2$-cochains over a loop $L$ as a natural extension of the usual $2$-cocycle identity, which allows the use of two distinct $2$-cochains. They form a vector space naturally isomorphic to the direct product of the space of $2$-cocycles and the space of $1$-cochains. This yields a generalization of the classical cocyclic construction of multidimensional Hadamard matrices from groups to arbitrary loops, while preserving the computational advantages of the cocyclic approach. The obtained decomposition shows that the determination of Hadamard equivalence classes can be reduced from the full space of $δ$-compatible pairs to the smaller space of ordinary $2$-cocycles, eliminating a redundant factor of $2^n$ from the search space. Computational results for all groups of orders $4$ and $8$, together with a non-associative loop of order $8$, show that the proposed three-dimensional construction refines the classical cocyclic Hadamard classification. While all examples collapse into a single equivalence class in dimension two, they split into several distinct classes in dimension three. In particular, the non-associative loop produces a three-dimensional Hadamard class that does not arise from any of the groups considered, showing that the multidimensional construction detects structural information that is invisible at the cocyclic matrix level.

</details>


### [136] [Exact Values, Extremal Classifications, and Sum-of-Squares Reductions for Second-Order Zarankiewicz Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18429)
*Yi Xu,Xihong Yan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将双二次型的SOS秩问题与C4-自由二部图的Zarankiewicz极值问题相联系，定义二阶Zarankiewicz数z2(m,n)，并通过分类低阶极值构型、有限穷举与轨道约简，证明了z2(4,4)=10、z2(7,4)=19、z2(8,4)=21、z2(5,5)=17，且这些情形下z2=zSL=zRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双二次型的SOS秩与C4-自由二部图Zarankiewicz极值问题存在自然联系：经典Zarankiewicz数z(m,n)对应单项平方的二部骨架，而允许两胞元构成双线性平方则引出"增广Zarankiewicz构型"与二阶Zarankiewicz数z2(m,n)。与通过递归充分条件定义的zRL、zSL不同，z2在所有不可约展示SOS分解上取最大值，且不施加(RW 3+)约束，因此证明z2(m,n)<=R必须证明所有展示平方更多的简单受限构型都可约，充分条件失效不构成反例。作者由此动机开展精确计算与分类。

Method: 方法上采用结构性链式策略：先分类低阶极值构型，再利用删除完全平方下的"遗传不可约性"约束下一阶。有限穷举步骤结合候选剪枝、必要相容图、团枚举与轨道约简，并对每个剩余轨道给出可验证的可约或不可约性证明。对5x5情形，先由有限排除将候选缩减到两个高对称的18平方候选，再构造显式九平方表示。

Result: 证明了z2(4,4)=10、z2(7,4)=19、z2(8,4)=21、z2(5,5)=17，且在这些情形下均有z2=zSL=zRL。6x4的极值不可约构型（16个展示平方）在行、列重标号下构成单一同构类；7x4的极值不可约构型（19个展示平方）恰有三个同构类。对5x5，两个普通极值骨架经有限排除后仅剩两个高度对称的18平方候选，它们定义相同的十平方多项式并存在显式九平方表示，从而给出z2(5,5)的上界。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了SOS秩与增广Zarankiewicz构型之间的精确联系，给出若干小阶z2(m,n)的精确值，并通过同构分类揭示了四列结果的结构性链式规律与5x5情形的强对称性。方法学上，分类-约简-轨道验证的框架为后续更高阶计算提供了可复用的技术路线。

Abstract: There is a natural connection between the SOS rank problem for bi-quadratic forms and the Zarankiewicz extremal problem for C4-free bipartite graphs. The classical Zarankiewicz number z(m,n) controls the bipartite skeleton associated with monomial squares. Allowing two cells to form a single bilinear square leads to augmented Zarankiewicz configurations and the second-order Zarankiewicz number z2(m,n). Unlike zRL and zSL, defined through recursive sufficient conditions, z2 maximizes over all irreducible displayed SOS decompositions without imposing (RW 3+). Hence, to prove z2(m,n)<=R, one must prove that every simple limited configuration with more displayed squares is reducible; failure of a sufficient condition cannot serve as a counterargument.
  We prove z2(4,4)=10, z2(7,4)=19, z2(8,4)=21, z2(5,5)=17, and obtain z2=zSL=zRL in all these cases. The extremal irreducible 6x4 configurations with 16 displayed squares form a single isomorphism class under row and column relabeling, whereas the extremal irreducible 7x4 configurations with 19 displayed squares form exactly three isomorphism classes. The four-column results form a structural chain: classify lower-order extremal configurations first, then use hereditary irreducibility under deletion of complete squares to constrain the next order. Finite exhaustive steps use candidate pruning, a necessary compatibility graph, clique enumeration, and orbit reduction, with a verifiable reducibility or irreducibility proof for each remaining orbit. For 5x5, there are two ordinary extremal skeletons; finite exclusion leaves only two highly symmetric 18-square candidates. They define the same ten-square polynomial, which admits an explicit nine-square representation, yielding the upper bound for z2(5,5).

</details>


### [137] [A Note on a Result of Chen Li](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18458)
*Xuan Fang,Tianyu Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文借助 C. Li 解决 Talagrand 离散凸性分数版问题的方法，回答了 Talagrand 提出的另一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Talagrand 曾提出若干离散凸性相关问题，其中分数版已被近期预印本解决，本文旨在沿用该方法处理另一个尚未解决的问题。

Method: 采用 C. Li 处理 Talagrand 离散凸性分数版问题所用的方法。

Result: 成功回答了 Talagrand 提出的另一个问题。

Conclusion: 表明 C. Li 的方法具有可迁移性，可用于解决 Talagrand 的其他相关问题。

Abstract: Recently, C. Li posted a preprint that resolves the fractional version of Talagrand's discrete convexity problem. In this note, we use the method of C. Li to answer another problem of Talagrand.

</details>


### [138] [Antidirected forests in digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18469)
*Gengtao Liu,Yunshu Gao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 反有向森林（无孤立点、含k条弧）在弧数超过阈值 g_k(n)=2max{C(2k-1,2), (k-1)(n-k/2)} 的有向图中必作为子有向图出现；n≥2k-1时该阈值等于 2ex(n,kK₂)，且极值由极值 kK₂-free 图的对称有向图达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究反有向森林（antidirected forest）在有向图中的极值嵌入问题，给出统一的弧数阈值，并刻画其极值与经典 Turán 型函数 ex(n,kK₂) 的联系。

Method: 证明结合三项工具：根反有向森林的计数不等式、扩展顶点不相交弧的嵌入方法、以及顶点删除；在剩余情形利用底层图的 Gallai–Edmonds 分解给出所需弧数界。

Result: 证明任意 n 阶、弧数大于 g_k(n)=2max{C(2k-1,2), (k-1)(n-k/2)} 的有向图 D 都包含 F 作为子有向图；当 n≥2k-1 时阈值恰为 2ex(n,kK₂)，由极值 kK₂-free 图对应的对称有向图达到。进而，所有此类森林的最大有向极值数为 2ex(n,kK₂)。

Conclusion: 反有向森林的有向嵌入阈值为 2ex(n,kK₂)（n≥2k-1），建立了有向极值数与无向 Turán 型极值数之间的对应关系。

Abstract: A digraph is antidirected if every vertex has indegree zero or outdegree zero. Let $k\ge2$, and let $F$ be an antidirected forest with $k$ arcs and no isolated vertices. We prove that every digraph $D$ of order $n$ with more than $g_k(n):=2\max\left\{\binom{2k-1}{2}, (k-1)\left(n-\frac{k}{2}\right)\right\}$ arcs contains $F$ as a subdigraph. For $n\ge2k-1$, this threshold equals $2\mathrm{ex}(n,kK_2)$ and is attained by symmetric digraphs arising from extremal $kK_2$-free graphs. Consequently, the maximum directed extremal number over all such forests is $2\mathrm{ex}(n,kK_2)$. The proof combines a counting inequality for rooted antidirected forests, embeddings extending vertex-disjoint arcs, and vertex deletion. In the remaining case, the Gallai--Edmonds decomposition of the underlying graph gives the required bound on the number of arcs.

</details>


### [139] [A somewhat sure note on an un-Schur problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18474)
*Swaroop Hegde,Hitesh Kumar,Pratibha*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文反驳了Parczyk与Spiegel关于3染色下彩虹Schur三元组最大占比的猜想，证明该占比渐近介于9/22与8/15之间，并推广至一般k染色情形给出新的非平凡界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parczyk与Spiegel开创了Schur定理的反Ramsey重数变体研究，证明3染色下彩虹Schur三元组最大占比渐近介于0.4与0.66364之间，并猜想其下界0.4最优。本文旨在检验并否定该猜想。

Method: 通过构造性证明与上界分析，对3染色下彩虹Schur三元组的最大占比进行重新界定，并将方法推广到一般k染色情形。

Result: 反驳了Parczyk-Spiegel猜想；证明3染色下彩虹Schur三元组最大占比渐近介于9/22与8/15之间（约0.4091与0.5333）；在一般k染色情形建立了新的非平凡上下界。

Conclusion: Parczyk与Spiegel的下界并非最优，本文给出了改进的渐近界，并拓展到k染色的一般框架。

Abstract: Parczyk and Spiegel initiated the study of an anti-Ramsey multiplicity variant of Schur's theorem and proved that the maximum fraction of Schur triples that can be rainbow in a $3$-coloring of $\{ 1, \dots ,n \}$ is bounded asymptotically between $0.4$ and $0.66364$. Furthermore, they conjectured that their lower bound is optimal. We disprove this conjecture and prove new bounds. In particular, we show that the maximum fraction of rainbow Schur triples that can be rainbow in a $3$-coloring of $\{ 1, \dots ,n \}$ lies between $9/22$ and $8/15$ asymptotically. Moreover, we study the problem in the general $k$-color setting and establish new non-trivial bounds.

</details>


### [140] [Finite and infinite barrycades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18476)
*Michał Dębski,Jarosław Grytczuk,Paweł Naroski,Bartłomiej Pawlik,Jakub Przybyło,Małgorzata Śleszyńska-Nowak*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明存在无穷多个规模 n 的 n-barricade 具有线性高度，并研究了无限变体，提出 greedy、grasshopper 和 precise grasshopper 三种构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2020 年 Richard Guy 提出 n-barricade 概念及 break-free n-barricade 的存在性问题：确定哪些 n 值存在覆盖 1 到 n(n+1)/2−1 所有前缀和的 barricade（此时高度应为 (n+2)/2）。

Method: 证明存在常数 c>0，使得无穷多个正整数 n 存在高度至少为 cn 的 n-barricade；同时研究问题的无限变体，提出 greedy、grasshopper、precise grasshopper 三种逐次改进的构造。

Result: 得到线性高度 barricade 的无穷存在性定理（高度 ≥ cn），并给出三种无限 barricade 构造，效果逐步提升；过程中发现了新的整数序列，并基于计算数据提出关于被省略元素、缺失部分和及无限 barricade 的单词表示等猜想。

Conclusion: barricade 在无穷多个规模上可达到线性高度；无限变体的构造方法得到改进，并引出若干新整数序列与猜想，为后续研究提供方向。

Abstract: An $n$-barrycade of height $h$ is a set of $h$ permutations of $[n]$ such that all the prefix sums are different. This notion was described in 2020 by Richard Guy, together with the central problem to determine for which values of $n$ there exists a break-free $n$-barrycade, that is, one in which every possible prefix sum from $1$ to $\frac{n(n+1)}{2}-1$ is covered. The height of such barrycade would be $\frac{n+2}{2}$.
  We prove that barrycades of linear height exist for infinitely many sizes: there is a constant $c>0$ such that for infinitely many positive integers $n$ there exists an $n$-barrycade of height at least $cn$. We also explore an infinite variant of the problem and propose three constructions -- greedy, grasshopper and precise grasshopper -- that give increasingly more satisfying results. Along the way we encounter new integer sequences and formulate conjectures concerning omitted elements, missing partial sums, and word representations of infinite barrycades, which are firmly supported by computational data.

</details>


### [141] [From Yang-Baxter to Robinson-Schensted-Knuth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18502)
*Leonid Petrov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文从Yang-Baxter方程出发推导RSK对应：对Schur权重的顶点模型，Yang-Baxter方程两侧的加项存在唯一的保权匹配，沿网格传播即得Fomin增长图形式的经典RSK；对Hall-Littlewood与q-Whittaker变形，确定性匹配失效，遂以概率耦合（双射化）取代匹配，得到输运概率测度的Markov算子，迭代生成随机RSK动力学与q-PushTASEP、随机六顶点模型等相互作用粒子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSK对应是代数组合中的核心双射，而Yang-Baxter方程源自量子多体系统并成为可解格模型（尤其顶点模型）的基石。作者希望揭示二者之间的内在统一机制：说明经典RSK可被视为特定顶点模型（配分函数为Schur多项式）上Yang-Baxter方程强制匹配沿网格传播的结果，从而为RSK提供可解模型视角的新解释，并推广到变形情形。

Method: 在顶点模型中，箭头占据网格边，每个顶点由四条边上箭头决定Boltzmann权，构型权为局部权之积，配分函数为给定边界条件下所有构型权之和。对配分函数为Schur多项式的顶点模型，Yang-Baxter方程的每一实例两侧加项之间存在唯一保权匹配；将该强制匹配沿网格传播，即得增长图形式的RSK。在自然坐标下，局部匹配化为三维R——Zamolodchikov四面体方程的组合、集合论解，其周期闭包给出单行晶体的组合R-矩阵。对Hall-Littlewood、q-Whittaker变形及其自旋版本，一般参数下不存在对全部边界数据有效的确定性匹配，故将Yang-Baxter方程每一实例概率化读取，用两侧的耦合（双射化/概率双射）替代匹配，得到输运顶点模型概率测度的Markov算子。

Result: 揭示了Schur情形下Yang-Baxter方程两侧的唯一保权匹配与Fomin增长图RSK的等价性，并给出其局部匹配的三维R（Zamolodchikov四面体方程的组合解）表述及单行晶体组合R-矩阵的周期闭包；对Hall-Littlewood与q-Whittaker变形及自旋版本，构造了概率双射化机制。迭代所得Markov算子可在网格上生成随机RSK型动力学与相互作用粒子系统，包括q-PushTASEP与随机六顶点模型。

Conclusion: Yang-Baxter方程提供了统一框架：Schur权重下产生确定性的RSK对应，而变形情形下确定匹配失效，需要借助概率双射化（耦合）将其推广为Markov型随机动力学。该视角把经典RSK、组合R-矩阵与随机相互作用粒子系统纳入同一可解模型体系，展示了可积性与概率组合之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We explain how to derive the Robinson-Schensted-Knuth (RSK) correspondence, a fundamental bijection in algebraic combinatorics, from the Yang-Baxter equation. The Yang-Baxter equation arose in the study of quantum many-body systems and later became a cornerstone of the theory of solvable lattice models, particularly vertex models. In a vertex model, arrows occupy the edges of a grid, and each vertex carries a Boltzmann weight determined by the arrows on the four edges meeting at it. The weight of a configuration is the product of these local weights, and a partition function is the sum of the weights of all configurations with prescribed boundary conditions. For a vertex model whose partition functions are the Schur polynomials, the two sides of each instance of the Yang-Baxter equation admit exactly one weight-preserving matching of their summands. Carried across a grid, this forced matching is the classical RSK correspondence in the form of Fomin's growth diagrams. In natural coordinates the local matching becomes the combinatorial three-dimensional R, a set-theoretic solution of the Zamolodchikov tetrahedron equation. Its periodic closure returns the combinatorial R-matrices of one-row crystals.
  The forced matching is special to the Schur weights. For the Hall-Littlewood and q-Whittaker deformations and their spin versions, at generic parameter values no deterministic matching works for all boundary data. Reading each instance of the Yang-Baxter equation probabilistically, we replace the matching by a coupling of the two sides - a bijectivization, or probabilistic bijection - and obtain Markov operators that transport probability measures attached to vertex models. Iterated over the grid, these operators produce randomized RSK-type dynamics and interacting particle systems, including q-PushTASEP and the stochastic six-vertex model.

</details>


### [142] [A proof of Chvátal's conjecture via a sharp correlation inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19123)
*Fan Chang,Hong Liu,Miao Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明Chvátal 1972年提出的猜想：有限集合上的每个遗传族都有最大的相交子族是星形族；更一般地，给出增布尔函数的尖锐相关性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chvátal于1972年提出的猜想长期未决，其相关性形式由Friedgut、Kahn、Kalai和Keller给出。本文旨在证明该猜想并建立更一般的尖锐相关性不等式。

Method: 对增布尔函数 f,g:{0,1}^n→{0,1}，设 g*(x)=1−g(1−x)，证明不等式：Σ_{∅≠S⊆[n]} ĝ(S)^2 max_{i∈S} Inf_i[f] ≤ 2Cov(f,g)Cov(f,g*)/(Cov(f,g)+Cov(f,g*))。当 g 为对踵函数（g=g*）时，导出 Cov(f,g)≥(1/4)min_i Inf_i[f]。

Result: 证明了对增布尔函数的尖锐相关性不等式，并作为其特例得到Chvátal猜想的相关性形式。

Conclusion: Chvátal猜想被证明成立：每个遗传族都有最大的星形相交子族；所建立的尖锐相关性不等式蕴含Friedgut–Kahn–Kalai–Keller的相关性表述。

Abstract: We prove Chvátal's conjecture, posed in 1972: every hereditary family of subsets of a finite set has a largest intersecting subfamily that is a star. More generally, we prove a sharp correlation inequality for increasing Boolean functions $f,g:\{0,1\}^n\to\{0,1\}$. Writing $g^*(x)=1-g(1-x)$, we show that $$ \sum_{\varnothing\ne S\subseteq[n]}\hat{g}(S)^2\max_{i\in S}\mathrm{Inf}_i[f]\le\frac{2\mathrm{Cov}(f,g)\mathrm{Cov}(f,g^*)}{\mathrm{Cov}(f,g)+\mathrm{Cov}(f,g^*)}. $$ When $g$ is antipodal, that is, $g=g^*$, this yields $\mathrm{Cov}(f,g)\ge\frac{1}{4}\min_{i\in[n]}\mathrm{Inf}_i[f]$, the correlation formulation of Chvátal's conjecture due to Friedgut, Kahn, Kalai and Keller.

</details>


### [143] [An inequality for the number of independent sets of matroids with an application to the forest-tree ratio of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18611)
*Ferenc Bencs,Péter Csikvári*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了拟阵中独立集总数与基数的比值下界不等式，并据此证明连通简单图的生成森林数与生成树数之比的最小值由完全图取到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拟阵独立集计数与基计数之间的定量关系，并将该组合不等式应用于图的生成森林与生成树计数之比，寻找该比值的最小情形。

Method: 以拟阵为框架：设每个秩 r-1 的独立集至少包含在 δ 个基中，通过计数与对数不等式推导 ln(I/I_r) ≥ (I_{r-1}/I_r)·δ·ln(1+1/δ)，进而结合若干其他思想（如完全图情形的比较与极值论证）证明图的结论。

Result: 若每个 F∈I_{r-1} 至少含于 δ 个基中，则 ln(I/I_r) ≥ (I_{r-1}/I_r)·δ·ln(1+1/δ)；特别地 I/I_r ≥ 2^{I_{r-1}/I_r}。对 n 顶点连通简单图 G，有 F(G)/T(G) ≥ F(K_n)/T(K_n)，等号当且仅当 G=K_n。

Conclusion: 建立了拟阵独立集与基数量比的一般下界，并给出图论推论：在 n 顶点连通简单图中，完全图 K_n 使生成森林数与生成树数之比达到最小，且等号仅由完全图取得。

Abstract: Let $M=(E,\mathcal{I})$ be a matroid of rank $r$. Let $\mathcal{I}_k$ be the independent sets of size $k$, and let $I_k=|\mathcal{I}_k|$ and $I=|\mathcal{I}|$. We show that if every set $F\in \mathcal{I}_{r-1}$ is contained in at least $δ$ bases, then $$\ln \left(\frac{I}{I_r}\right)\geqslant \frac{I_{r-1}}{I_r}\cdot δ\ln \left(1+\frac{1}δ\right).$$ In particular, we have $$\frac{I}{I_r}\geqslant 2^{I_{r-1}/I_r}.$$ By combining this result with several other ideas, we prove that if $G$ is a simple connected graph on $n$ vertices, and $F(G)$ and $T(G)$ denote its numbers of spanning forests and spanning trees, respectively, then $$\frac{F(G)}{T(G)}\geqslant \frac{F(K_n)}{T(K_n)},$$ where $K_n$ is the complete graph on $n$ vertices. Equality holds if and only if $G=K_n$.

</details>


### [144] [A Hajnal-Szemerédi Theorem for Skewed Colorings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18629)
*Mathis Birken*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hajnal-Szemerédi定理在一般指定颜色类大小下的推广版本，仅需满足一个自然上界条件即可实现公平着色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hajnal-Szemerédi定理仅保证最大度不超过r的图存在公平的(r+1)-着色（即各颜色类大小尽量均等）。然而许多应用场景需要更一般的、预先指定的颜色类大小，而非严格的均等分配。本文旨在将此定理推广到更一般的指定颜色类大小情形。

Method: 通过图论与组合方法，在最大度至多r的图上，对任意满足一个自然上界条件的指定颜色类大小序列，构造并证明存在符合该大小分配的(r+1)-着色。

Result: 证明了对更一般的指定颜色类大小，只要满足一个自然上界，最大度不超过r的图均存在相应的(r+1)-着色，从而推广了Hajnal-Szemerédi定理。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典公平着色定理拓展至更灵活的着色容量设定，为相关组合与算法问题提供了更一般的理论保证。

Abstract: The Hajnal-Szemerédi theorem guarantees an equitable $(r + 1)$-coloring of every graph of maximum degree at most $r$. We prove a version of this result for more general prescribed color class sizes, subject only to a natural upper bound.

</details>


### [145] [Sim-Width, Induced Matching Treewidth, and Tree-Independence Number in Induced $K_{t,t}$-Free Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18648)
*Mengyuan Niu,Xiumei Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了树独立数 $tree\text{-}\alpha(G)$ 与 sim-width、诱导匹配树宽等图参数之间的关系，证明了对诱导 $K_{t,t}$-free 图的两类多项式上界，正面回答了 Abrishami 等人及 Alon 等人提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树独立数、诱导匹配树宽与 sim-width 均基于树分解或分支分解定义。Abrishami 等（SIDMA 2025）和 Brettell 等（EJC 2025）询问有界 sim-width 是否在排除诱导 $K_{t,t}$ 的条件下蕴含树独立数有界；Alon 等（arXiv 2025）则询问固定诱导匹配树宽时，树独立数是否关于 $t$ 多项式有界。本文旨在回答这些公开问题，并改进相关已知结果。

Method: 针对 sim-width 情形，证明对整数 $t\geq 2$、$s\geq 1$，任意诱导 $K_{t,t}$-free 且 $simw(G)\leq s$ 的图满足 $tree\text{-}\alpha(G)=O_t((s+1)^{2t^2-2t})$。针对诱导匹配树宽情形，使用 VC-维数（VC-dimension）论证，证明对整数 $\mu\geq 1$、$t\geq 2$，任意诱导 $K_{t,t}$-free 且 $tree\text{-}\mu(G)\leq\mu$ 的图满足 $tree\text{-}\alpha(G)=t^{O_\mu(1)}$。

Result: 证明了两个多项式上界：其一，诱导 $K_{t,t}$-free 且 sim-width 有界的图，其树独立数被 $(s+1)$ 的多项式所界；其二，诱导 $K_{t,t}$-free 且诱导匹配树宽有界的图，其树独立数关于 $t$ 多项式有界。前者将 Alon 等定理的指数从 $3t^2+1$ 降至 $2t^2-2t$，并给出了 Bešter Štorgel 等关于诱导 $K_{1,t}$-free 图猜想的多项式强化形式。

Conclusion: 有界 sim-width 结合排除诱导 $K_{t,t}$ 确实蕴含树独立数有界，从而正面回答了 Abrishami 等人与 Brettell 等人的问题；固定诱导匹配树宽时树独立数亦关于 $t$ 多项式有界，正面回答了 Alon 等人的问题。两项结果共同刻画了这些参数间的多项式依赖关系。

Abstract: The tree-independence number $tree\text{-}α(G)$, the induced matching treewidth $tree\text{-}μ(G)$, and the sim-width $simw(G)$ are graph parameters defined in terms of tree or branch decompositions. We establish two polynomial bounds for the tree-independence number of induced $K_{t,t}$-free graphs, one in terms of sim-width and the other in terms of induced matching treewidth. Abrishami et al. (SIDMA, 2025) and Brettell et al. (EJC, 2025) asked whether bounded sim-width, together with the exclusion of an induced $K_{t,t}$, implies bounded tree-independence number. We answer this question by proving that, for integers $t\geq 2$ and $s\geq 1$, every induced $K_{t,t}$-free graph $G$ with $simw(G)\leq s$ satisfies $tree\text{-}α(G)=O_t\left((s+1)^{2t^2-2t}\right)$. This also proves a polynomial strengthening of a conjecture of Bešter Štorgel et al. (arXiv, 2026) concerning induced $K_{1,t}$-free graphs and improves a theorem of Alon et al. (arXiv, 2025) by reducing the exponent from $3t^2+1$ to $2t^2-2t$. Alon et al. (arXiv, 2025) asked whether, for fixed induced matching treewidth, the tree-independence number is polynomially bounded in $t$. Using a VC-dimension argument, we answer this question affirmatively by showing that, for integers $μ\geq 1$ and $t\geq 2$, every induced $K_{t,t}$-free graph $G$ with $tree\text{-}μ(G)\leqμ$ satisfies $tree\text{-}α(G)=t^{O_μ(1)}$.

</details>


### [146] [Sharp Rainbow Path Covers in Dense and Complete Multipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18740)
*Xiao-Chuan Liu,Boyan Xu,Xu Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究图论中"彩虹路径覆盖"问题，证明稠密图在任意正常边染色下可用约 n/2 条彩虹路径覆盖全部边，并渐近确定完全多部图的 rpc 值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩虹路径覆盖（rainbow path cover）是边染色图中的自然覆盖问题。此前对稠密图在任意正常边染色下需要多少条彩虹路径覆盖所有边（即 rpc(G) 的上界）以及完全多部图等具体图类的渐近值缺乏精确刻画，本文旨在给出稠密图的最优渐近上界并确定完全多部图的 rpc。

Method: 证明结合了三种工具：（1）全局彩虹线性森林（globally rainbow linear forests）的伪随机填充；（2）将稠密图分解为稠密部并施加任意稠密图的指定规避（prescribed avoidance）；（3）对完全多部图使用预留连接子（reserved connectors）与直接的主部（dominant-part）论证。

Result: 主要结果：对任意固定 0<α<1，每个最小度至少为 αn 的 n 顶点正常边染色图满足 rpc(G,c) ≤ (1+o(1))n/2，且系数 1/2 最优。此外，对所有完全多部图渐近确定了 rpc(G)：若 G=K_{n_1,…,n_r} 有 n 个顶点、最大与最小部大小为 M 和 s，则一致地有 rpc(G)=(1+o(1))max{min{⌊n/2⌋, n−M}, (n−s)/2}。

Conclusion: 论文完全刻画了稠密图彩虹路径覆盖数的渐近上界（n/2 且最优），并给出了完全多部图 rpc 的统一渐近公式，推广并统一了此前相关结果。

Abstract: A path in a properly edge-colored graph is rainbow if its edges have pairwise distinct colors. For a proper edge-coloring $c$ of a graph $G$, let $\operatorname{rpc}(G,c)$ be the minimum number of rainbow paths needed to cover $E(G)$, and let $\operatorname{rpc}(G)$ be the maximum of $\operatorname{rpc}(G,c)$ over all proper edge-colorings of $G$. We prove that, for every fixed $0<α<1$, every properly edge-colored $n$-vertex graph with minimum degree at least $αn$ satisfies $\operatorname{rpc}(G,c)\leq(1+o(1))n/2$, where the coefficient $1/2$ is best possible. We also determine $\operatorname{rpc}(G)$ asymptotically for every complete multipartite graph. If $G=K_{n_1,\ldots,n_r}$ has order $n$ and largest and smallest part sizes $M$ and $s$, respectively, then, uniformly over all choices of the number and sizes of the parts, $\operatorname{rpc}(G)=(1+o(1))\max\{\min\{\lfloor n/2\rfloor,n-M\},(n-s)/2\}$. The proof combines pseudorandom packings of globally rainbow linear forests with a decomposition into dense parts and prescribed avoidance for arbitrary dense graphs, and with reserved connectors and a direct dominant-part argument for complete multipartite graphs.

</details>


### [147] [Solution to a conjecture on integral uniform hypercycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18816)
*Joyentanuj Das,Iswar Mahato*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了 $k$-一致超圈 $\Cnk$ 的邻接特征值为整数（即整谱）的充要条件，证明了 Portugal 与 Del-Vecchio 提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Portugal 和 Del-Vecchio 近期研究了整超图，并刻画了 $3$、$4$、$5$-一致超圈的整谱情形，同时猜想：当 $n>6$ 且 $k<n-1$ 时，$k$-一致超圈 $\Cnk$ 永不整谱。本文旨在证实该猜想并给出完整的整谱刻画。

Method: 首先计算 $\Cnk$ 的完整邻接谱，然后综合运用 Niven 定理、分圆单位引理、欧拉 $\varphi$ 函数的一个初等性质，以及分圆域的 Galois 对称性完成证明。

Result: 证明了对 $2\le k\le n-1$，$\Cnk$ 整谱当且仅当 $k=n-1$ 或 $(n,k)\in\{(4,2),(6,2),(6,3),(6,4)\}$，从而证实了原猜想。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 $n$ 个顶点上 $k$-一致整超圈的完整刻画。

Abstract: A hypergraph is said to be integral if all of its adjacency eigenvalues are integers. Recently, Portugal and Del-Vecchio in [\emph{Appl. Math. Comput.} 504: 129507 (2025)] studied the integral hypergraphs and gave a characterization of integral hypercycles in three particular cases: $3$-uniform, $4$-uniform and $5$-uniform hypercycles. In the same article, they conjectured that the $k$-uniform hypercycle on $n$ vertices $\Cnk$ is never integral for $k<n-1$, when $n>6$. In this article, we confirm this conjecture and prove that for $2\le k\le n-1$, $\Cnk$ is integral if and only if $k=n-1$ or $(n,k)\in\{(4,2),(6,2),(6,3),(6,4)\}$. The proof begins with computing the complete adjacency spectrum of $\Cnk$ and then uses Niven's theorem, a cyclotomic-unit lemma, an elementary property of Euler's totient function, and the Galois symmetry of cyclotomic fields to complete it. Our result gives a complete characterization of $k$-uniform integral hypercycles on $n$ vertices.

</details>


### [148] [A non-trivial bound for 3AP-intersecting families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18870)
*Peter Keevash*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了3AP-intersecting族的大小上界为(1/2 - c)2^n，是Simonovits-Sós猜想（最大规模2^{n-3}）的首个非平凡进展，并将结果推广到有界余度3-图H的H-intersecting族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simonovits和Sós猜想：若[ n ]的子集族中任意两个成员的交集包含一个非平凡三项等差数列（3AP），则该族最大可能大小为2^{n-3}。此前该猜想缺乏非平凡进展，本文旨在给出首个实质性的上界结果。

Method: 将问题置于H-intersecting框架下，其中H是[ n ]上的3-图且具有有界余度（bounded codegrees）；在此推广设定下证明大小上界(1/2 - c)2^n。同时通过clique构造说明有界余度假设不可去除。

Result: 存在绝对常数c>0，使得任何3AP-intersecting族F满足|F| ≤ (1/2 - c)2^n；对有界余度3-图的H-intersecting族同样成立。Clique构造表明有界余度条件不可省略。

Conclusion: 给出了Simonovits-Sós猜想的首个非平凡上界进展，并推广到有界余度3-图的H-intersecting族；同时证明了该推广的紧性依赖于有界余度假设。

Abstract: A family $F$ of subsets of $[n]$ is 3AP-intersecting if every two members have intersection containing a non-trivial three-term arithmetic progression. We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that any such $F$ has size at most $(\tfrac12 - c)2^n$. This is the first non-trivial progress towards a conjecture of Simonovits and Sós that the maximum possible size is $2^{n-3}$. More generally, we show the same bound for $H$-intersecting families whenever $H$ is a $3$-graph on $[n]$ with bounded codegrees. A clique shows that this is sharp, in that the bounded codegree assumption cannot be removed.

</details>


### [149] [Decomposing Gorenstein polytopes of large index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18873)
*Johannes Knupfer,Benjamin Nill*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Gorenstein多胞体的free-join分解定理，作为Batyrev-Juny格锥定理的强推广，并据此验证了关于stringy E-多项式的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Batyrev-Borger-Haase-Kretschmer-Payne等前人关于Gorenstein多胞体分解的结果，并进一步推广Batyrev-Juny格锥定理，同时解决Batyrev与作者关于stringy E-多项式的猜想。

Method: 证明Gorenstein多胞体的free-join分解定理；对指数大于(d+2)/2的d维Gorenstein多胞体给出free join分解；对Batyrev-Juny猜想极端情形给出证明（见第一作者硕士论文）。部分证明借助ChatGPT 5.6 Sol发现。

Result: 任何指数大于(d+2)/2的d维Gorenstein多胞体P可表示为Gorenstein多胞体的free join；Gorenstein多胞体P的stringy E-多项式为零当且仅当P是thin的（即其局部h*-多项式为零），否则具有预期次数。

Conclusion: 该分解定理显著强化了已有结果并推广Batyrev-Juny格锥定理，同时验证了Batyrev与第二作者关于stringy E-多项式的猜想。

Abstract: In this paper we prove a free-join decomposition theorem on Gorenstein polytopes significantly improving upon results in prior joint work of the second author with Batyrev, Borger, Haase, Kretschmer, and Payne. In particular, as a strong extension of the Batyrev-Juny lattice pyramid theorem it implies that any $d$-dimensional Gorenstein polytope $P$ of index larger than $\frac{d+2}{2}$ is a free join of Gorenstein polytopes. Here, the index (also called codegree) is the dilation factor $r$ such that $rP$ is reflexive. As our main application, we prove that the stringy $E$-polynomial of a Gorenstein polytope $P$ vanishes if and only if $P$ is thin (i.e., its local $h^*$-polynomial vanishes), and it has the expected degree otherwise. The latter was conjectured by Batyrev and the second author.
  The proofs of the general results were found via ChatGPT 5.6 Sol, while a proof of the extremal case of the Batyrev-Juny conjecture is contained in the master thesis of the first author.

</details>


### [150] [Connected Mutual-Visibility in Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18877)
*Tonny K B,Shikhi M*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 引入连通互见数 μ_c(G)，研究其结构性质与算法复杂度，给出界、Nordhaus–Gaddum 不等式、极值图刻画、正则图结果、块结构局部确定性质，并证明判定问题 NP-完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在互见集（mutual-visibility set）这一图论概念基础上，进一步要求集合诱导子图连通，提出连通互见数 μ_c(G)，以刻画兼具可见性与连通性的顶点集合的极值与算法特性。

Method: 定义连通互见集：S 中任意两点间存在内部顶点在 S 外的最短路，且 G[S] 连通；定义 μ_c(G) 为最大基数。建立基本界，推导 Nordhaus–Gaddum 型不等式，刻画达到最小/最大值的图；对正则 (d,2,-δ)-图给出一般界并确定两个亏量 2 的三次图的精确值；证明 μ_c(G) 由 G 的块结构局部决定，即等于各块对应值的最大值；给出识别连通互见集的多项式时间算法，并证明相关判定问题 NP-完全。

Result: 得到 μ_c(G) 的基本界与 Nordhaus–Gaddum 型不等式；刻画极值图；对正则 (d,2,-δ)-图给出一般界并确定两个亏量 2 三次图的精确值；证明 μ_c(G) 等于其各块上对应值的最大值；给出识别连通互见集的多项式时间算法；证明对应判定问题即使对直径不超过 4 的连通二部图也是 NP-完全的。

Conclusion: 连通互见数 μ_c(G) 具有良好的结构刻画（局部由块结构决定）与极值理论，但其识别/判定在算法上具有计算复杂性障碍，即使限制到特定图类仍 NP-完全。

Abstract: A set $S$ of vertices of a graph $G$ is a connected mutual-visibility set if every two vertices of $S$ are joined by a shortest path whose internal vertices lie outside $S$, and the subgraph induced by $S$ is connected. We introduce the connected mutual-visibility number $μ_c(G)$, defined as the maximum cardinality of such a set, and investigate its structural and algorithmic properties. We establish fundamental bounds, derive Nordhaus--Gaddum type inequalities, and characterise the graphs attaining the minimum and maximum possible values. For regular $(d,2,-δ)$-graphs, we derive general bounds on $μ_c(G)$ and determine its exact value for the two cubic graphs of defect $2$. We further show that $μ_c(G)$ is determined locally by the block structure of $G$, namely, it is equal to the maximum of the corresponding values over the blocks of $G$. Finally, we present a polynomial-time algorithm for recognising connected mutual-visibility sets and prove that the associated decision problem is $\mathsf{NP}$-complete, even for connected bipartite graphs of diameter at most $4$.

</details>


### [151] [Transfer of difference structures: a new semidirect product framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18897)
*Sophie Huczynska,Struan McCartney,Carys Williams*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于半直积的显式框架，用于在群之间转移差集、外部差族与近因子分解等差结构，涵盖经典结果并解决Swartz等人的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差集、外部差族与近因子分解是群中满足差或和均匀性条件的结构，研究长期以交换群为中心，但存在连接交换与非交换结构的经典结果（如Dillon二面体技巧、Pêcher关于近因子分解的工作），近期重新受到关注。作者希望建立统一框架以转移这些差结构。

Method: 采用半直积方法，重点研究半直积$G \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$，构建显式框架，实现从交换群到非交换群以及不同非交换群之间的差结构转移；该框架可处理$\lambda$-重近因子分解、差集以及任意多集合的外部差结构。

Result: 框架涵盖并统一了若干已有结果，解决了Swartz等人的一个公开问题；获得了多类群中的新近因子分解与差结构，并能转移此前方法无法转移的例子；建立了一个新的三集合交换循环外部差族无限族，并由此构造出首个非交换循环外部差族无限族。

Conclusion: 半直积框架为群差结构的转移提供了通用工具，不仅统一了经典与近期结果，还产生了新的构造与无限族，将差结构研究从交换群有效拓展到非交换群。

Abstract: Difference sets, external difference families and near-factorizations of groups are much-studied structures satisfying certain uniformity conditions on the differences or sums within or between elements of sets in groups. Research has centred on abelian groups, though there are classic results (such as Dillon's Dihedral Trick for difference sets and the work of Pêcher on near-factorizations) connecting abelian and non-abelian structures, which have recently regained attention. We present a new explicit framework enabling transfer of difference structures between groups using a semidirect product approach, establishing new tools and constructions, encompassing various previous results and addressing an open problem of Swartz et al. Motivated by the classic dihedral results, we focus on semidirect products $G \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$. Transfer from abelian to non-abelian groups, and between distinct non-abelian groups, are both possible, and our framework can handle $λ$-fold near-factorizations, difference sets and external difference structures with arbitrarily many sets. We obtain new near-factorizations and difference structures in a range of groups, and can transfer various examples not transferrable by previous approaches. We showcase our approach by establishing a new infinite family of three-set abelian circular external difference families, then producing from this the first infinite family of non-abelian circular external difference families.

</details>


### [152] [Rational values and non-principality for $\ell$-degree Turán densities of hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18927)
*Quanyu Tang,Wenling Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：当 k>ℓ>k/2 时，任意有理数 α∈[0,1) 都可作为某个有限 k-图族的 ℓ-度 Turán 密度；对任意 k>ℓ≥1，构造出单禁止 k-图实现 γ=1-q^{ℓ-k} 的无穷序列；并建立 ℓ-度 Turán 密度的强化非主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ-度 Turán 密度是超图 Turán 理论中刻画 ℓ-度而非边数的核心参数。论文旨在回答该密度函数的取值集合问题（可实现性），并探究其是否具有与经典 Turán 密度类似的组合性质（如非主性）。

Method: 通过构造性地设计有限 k-图族与单个 k-图，结合极值组合与度数归一化分析，证明有理数可实现性及具体值序列，并利用两个 k-图叠加构造给出非主性不等式。

Result: 对 k>ℓ>k/2，每个有理数 α∈[0,1) 均可实现为某有限族的 ℓ-度 Turán 密度；对任意 k>ℓ≥1 及整数 q≥2，存在 k-图 F 使 γ^{(k)}_ℓ(F)=1-q^{ℓ-k}；且对 k>ℓ>1，存在 F_1,F_2 使得 0<γ^{(k)}_ℓ({F_1,F_2})<min{γ^{(k)}_ℓ(F_1),γ^{(k)}_ℓ(F_2)}。

Conclusion: ℓ-度 Turán 密度取值丰富，可覆盖 [0,1) 中全部有理数（特定参数范围），并可由单个禁止图实现显式离散值序列；同时其非主性比经典情形更强，说明 ℓ-度 Turán 密度不具备简单的主性结构。

Abstract: Let $k>\ell\ge 1$ be integers. For a family $\mathcal{F}$ of $k$-uniform hypergraphs ($k$-graphs), the $\ell$-degree Turán density $γ^{(k)}_\ell(\mathcal{F})$ of $\mathcal{F}$ is defined as the asymptotic maximum of the normalized minimum $\ell$-degree over all $\mathcal{F}$-free $k$-graphs.
  In this paper, we prove that for all $k>\ell>k/2$, every rational number $α\in[0,1)$ can be realized as the $\ell$-degree Turán density $γ^{(k)}_\ell(\mathcal{F})$ for some finite family $\mathcal{F}$ of $k$-graphs. Furthermore, for any $k>\ell\ge 1$, we construct an explicit infinite sequence of values realized by single forbidden $k$-graphs: for each integer $q\ge2$, there exists a $k$-graph $F$ such that $γ^{(k)}_\ell(F)=1-q^{\ell-k}$. We also establish a strengthened non-principality property for $\ell$-degree Turán densities: for all $k>\ell>1$, there exist two $k$-graphs $F_1$ and $F_2$ satisfying $0< γ^{(k)}_{\ell}(\{F_1,F_2\})<\min\{ γ^{(k)}_\ell(F_1),γ^{(k)}_\ell(F_2)\}$.

</details>


### [153] [Bounded VC-dimension implies the Erdős--Rado sunflower conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18995)
*Gennian Ge,Jian Wang,Zixiang Xu,Xiaochen Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文在有界VC维的集合族上证明了Erdős–Rado太阳花猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős–Rado太阳花猜想是极值组合论中长期未解的重要问题，本文旨在对该猜想在具有有界VC维的受限集合族情形下给出证明。

Method: 针对$\ell$-uniform集合族，在全族成员数超过$K(r)^{\ell}$的条件下，结合VC维有界的结构性限制进行组合分析，证明其中必然包含$r$-太阳花。

Result: 证明了：对任意固定整数$r\ge 3$，存在常数$K(r)$，使得成员数超过$K(r)^{\ell}$且VC维有界的$\ell$-uniform集合族必定包含一个$r$-太阳花。即在该受限族类上太阳花猜想成立。

Conclusion: 太阳花猜想在具有有界VC维的集合族上成立，表明VC维有界这一结构性条件足以保证猜想的成立。

Abstract: The Erdős--Rado sunflower conjecture asserts that, for every fixed integer $r\ge 3$, there is a constant $K(r)$ such that every $\ell$-uniform family with more than $K(r)^{\ell}$ members contains an $r$-sunflower. We prove this conjecture for families of bounded VC-dimension.

</details>


### [154] [Characteristic drops for high-order vanishing on the hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19009)
*Dean Menezes*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文确定了在布尔立方体非零顶点处以至少 k 阶消失、在原点处以恰好 ℓ 阶消失的多项式的最小次数，并给出了显式基与特征依赖的精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究布尔立方体上消失理想（vanishing ideal）中带指定原点重数的极小次数多项式，刻画其最小次数如何随域 F 的特征变化。这一问题联系到 Reed–Muller 码、Catalan 数非零性以及消失空间的约化结构。

Method: 设 F 为域，0≤ℓ≤k−2，n≥k−1。考察在布尔立方体每个非零顶点处以至少 k 阶消失、在原点处以恰好 ℓ 阶消失的多项式空间，求其最小次数；构造约化消失空间的显式基，证明在该基下最高次映射为对角映射且对角元为 Catalan 数；借助块坐标（block coordinates）的精化证明该基与多项式次数相容；并用正整数分解刻画 Catalan 数在 F 中何时非零。

Result: 最小次数为 n+2k−2−ρ_F(k−ℓ)，其中 ρ_F(s) 为使对应 Catalan 数在 F 中非零的正整数之和等于 s−1 的最少项数。奇特征下，该次数为 n+2k−3 或 n+2k−4，取决于 C_{k−ℓ−2} 是否非零；特征 2 下为 n+2k−2−s_2(k−ℓ−1)，s_2 为二进制数位和。由此首次特征下降及后续所有下降均被精确确定。

Conclusion: 论文给出了布尔立方体消失空间中指定原点重数多项式最小次数的完整特征依赖公式，通过显式基、对角化最高次映射与块坐标相容性完成证明，并精确确定奇特征与特征 2 下的下降行为。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a field, let $0\le \ell\le k-2$, and suppose that $n\ge k-1$. We determine the minimum degree of a polynomial in $F[x_1,\ldots,x_n]$ that vanishes to order at least $k$ at every nonzero vertex of the Boolean cube and to order exactly $\ell$ at the origin. The answer is \[
  n+2k-2-ρ_F(k-\ell), \] where $ρ_F(s)$ is the least number of positive integers summing to $s-1$ whose corresponding Catalan numbers are nonzero in $F$.
  The proof gives an explicit basis of the reduced vanishing space. In this basis the top-degree map is diagonal, with Catalan numbers on the diagonal; a refinement using block coordinates shows that the basis is compatible with polynomial degree. In odd characteristic the degree is either $n+2k-3$ or $n+2k-4$, according as $C_{k-\ell-2}$ is nonzero or zero. In characteristic $2$ it is \[
  n+2k-2-s_2(k-\ell-1), \] where $s_2$ denotes binary digit sum. Thus the first characteristic drop, and all later drops, are determined exactly.

</details>


### [155] [A Direct Proof of the Locally Dense Graphon Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19014)
*Dean Menezes*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文给出了Bradač–Sudakov–Wigderson关于p-局部稠密图极限(graphon)二次不等式刻画的一个直接证明，通过简单函数的随机取整代替Reiher有限引理与graphon逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bradač、Sudakov与Wigderson用对全体有界非负函数的二次不等式刻画了p-局部稠密graphon，但其证明依赖Reiher有限引理与graphon逼近，并公开征询直接证明。本文动机即提供一个不依赖该引理的直接证明，并澄清结论中无原子性假设的必要性。

Method: 对简单函数进行随机取整：将每个水平集(level set)等测度地划分为m份，各份以对应水平值作为概率独立保留。由此构造的随机简单函数的非对角项期望与原函数一致，对角项误差不超过 ‖W−p‖_∞/(4m)。令m→∞，再在L¹中逼近一般函数，从而得到所需二次不等式。另构造三原子(atoms)例子。

Result: 成功给出二次不等式的直接证明；并证明若允许任意概率空间，则"无原子性"是必要的——三原子反例表明结论在该情形下不成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为p-局部稠密graphon的二次刻画提供了一个更初等、无需Reiher有限引理与graphon逼近的直接证明，同时指出无原子性在一般概率空间框架下的必要性。

Abstract: Bradač, Sudakov, and Wigderson characterized $p$-locally dense graphons by a quadratic inequality for all bounded nonnegative functions. Their proof uses Reiher's finite lemma and graphon approximation, and they asked for a direct proof. We give one by rounding simple functions. Divide each level set into $m$ equal-measure pieces and retain each piece independently with probability equal to its level. Off-diagonal terms agree in expectation; the diagonal error is at most $\norminf{W-p}/(4m)$. Letting $m\to\infty$ and then approximating in $L^1$ proves the inequality. A three-atom example shows that atomlessness is necessary if arbitrary probability spaces are allowed.

</details>


### [156] [Robust ladders in the HLM tower construction for arithmetic regularity over ${\bf F}_2^{\,n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19015)
*Dean Matthew Menezes*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Green算术正则引理在 $G=\mathbf{F}_2^n$ 上的tower型下界，证明了HLMS构造中的order property在对抗性编辑下具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Green算术正则引理在 $\mathbf{F}_2^n$ 上存在tower型下界，理解其构造的稳定性对刻画正则性下界的本质具有重要意义。论文旨在证明HLMS构造所依赖的order property在对抗性编辑下依然鲁棒，从而强化下界结论并揭示其对周期逼近的阻碍。

Method: 设 $f=s^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{s}\mathbf 1_{A_i}$，$s=\lfloor1/(16\varepsilon)\rfloor$，$d_s$ 为顶层块维数。定义集合 $A$ 中长度为 $r$ 的ladder为满足 $a_p+b_q\in A \iff p\le q$ 的模式。对任意满足构造spanning条件的HLM steering system，改变 $A_s$ 至多 $|G|/16$ 个点仍保留长度 $d_s/8$ 的ladder。证明将 $2^{d_s}$ 个行迹视为长度 $8d_s$、相对距离大于 $1/4$ 的二进制码，再应用Sauer引理。此外选取顶层steering map使得 $f$ 的上水平集在至多 $\varepsilon|G|$ 点的任意编辑后仍保留长度至少 $d_s/(8\sqrt{s})\ge\mathrm{twr}(s-2)$ 的ladder。

Result: 证明了HLMS构造的order property在对抗性编辑下鲁棒：编辑 $|G|/16$ 点后仍保留长度 $d_s/8$ 的ladder；编辑 $\varepsilon|G|$ 点后上水平集仍保留长度至少 $\mathrm{twr}(s-2)$ 的ladder。鲁棒ladder进一步阻碍低指数周期逼近，且足够高的Green正则性代价迫使与每个固定stable class具有正的编辑距离。

Conclusion: 论文表明Green算术正则引理的tower型下界具有强鲁棒性：即使经过对抗性编辑，HLMS构造中的order property依然保留ladder结构，从而阻碍低指数周期逼近，并说明有成本的Green正则性与任何固定stable class之间存在正编辑距离。

Abstract: Green's arithmetic regularity lemma over $G={\bf F}_2^{\,n}$ has tower-type lower bounds. We show that the order property in the Hosseini--Lovett--Moshkovitz--Shapira construction is robust under adversarial edits. Write $f=s^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{s}\mathbf 1_{A_i}$, where $s=\lfloor1/(16ε)\rfloor$, and let $d_s$ be the top block dimension. A ladder of length $r$ in a set $A$ is a pattern $a_p+b_q\in A$ exactly when $p\le q$. For every HLM steering system satisfying the construction's spanning conditions, changing $A_s$ on at most $|G|/16$ points leaves a ladder of length $d_s/8$. The proof views the $2^{d_s}$ row traces as a binary code of length $8d_s$ and relative distance greater than $1/4$, then applies Sauer's lemma. We also choose the top steering map so that a superlevel set of $f$ retains a ladder of length at least $d_s/(8\sqrt{s})\ge{\rm twr}(s-2)$ after every edit on at most $ε|G|$ points. Finally, robust ladders obstruct low-index periodic approximation, and sufficiently costly Green regularity forces positive edit distance from every fixed stable class.

</details>


### [157] [Maximum cut and maximum bisection in random regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19057)
*P. M. Aronow,Patrick Lopatto*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明随机d-正则图的最大割（MaxCut）与最大二分（MaxBis）期望之差为o(n)，即强制割两侧等大小仅使最优割期望值改变亚线性条边；两者极限密度相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机d-正则图上MaxCut的期望值研究较深入，而MaxBis（两侧大小必须相等的最大割）受关注较少。二者是否具有相同的极限密度此前尚不清楚，本文旨在量化等大小约束对最优割的影响。

Method: 先在配置模型上证明：将n个顶点图上每种可能基数的割，与一个相关的2n顶点图的二分进行对比；关键工具是Huang插值定理（2018），为此建立单条边加入时最优割变化的结构性质；再结合集中估计与Huang关于最大二分（MaxBis）的收敛定理；最后通过对配置模型施加"简单图"条件，转回均匀随机简单d-正则图。

Result: 对固定的次数d与偶数顶点数n>d，均匀随机简单d-正则图G_{n,d}满足 E[MaxCut(G_{n,d})] − E[MaxBis(G_{n,d})] = o(n)。

Conclusion: 要求割的两侧恰好等大小，只会使期望最优割改变亚线性条边；MaxCut与MaxBis具有相同的极限密度。

Abstract: We prove that, for every fixed degree $d$ and a uniformly random simple $d$-regular graph $G_{n,d}$ on an even number $n>d$ of vertices, $$\mathbb{E}\operatorname{MaxCut}(G_{n,d})-\mathbb{E}\operatorname{MaxBis}(G_{n,d})=o(n).$$ In other words, requiring the two sides of a cut to have exactly the same size changes the expected optimal cut by only a sublinear number of edges. We prove this first for the configuration model by comparing cuts of each possible cardinality on an $n$-vertex graph with bisections of a related graph on $2n$ vertices. An important technical tool is Huang's interpolation theorem (Huang, 2018); to apply it, we establish a structural property of the change in the optimal cut when a single edge is added. This comparison, together with concentration estimates and Huang's convergence theorem for maximum bisection, shows that maximum cut and maximum bisection have the same limiting density. Conditioning the configuration model on being simple then gives the result for uniformly random simple regular graphs.

</details>


### [158] [Simple Cayley permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19078)
*Giulio Cerbai,Anders Claesson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出与膨胀运算相容的Cayley排列简单性概念，证明其具有类的置换替换分解，并据此计数简单Cayley排列及若干子类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置换理论中简单置换与替换分解是核心工具，但Cayley排列（允许重复元素的置换推广）尚缺与之相容的简单性定义。作者希望建立类似框架以刻画相应结构类。

Method: 定义与膨胀相容的Cayley排列简单性；证明Cayley排列存在类比于置换的替换分解；利用该分解对简单Cayley排列、本原简单Cayley排列及简单限制增长函数进行计数；并分析有限多个简单成员的遗传Cayley排列类。

Result: 给出简单Cayley排列、本原简单Cayley排列及简单限制增长函数的计数结果；证明任一仅含有限多个简单成员的遗传Cayley排列类具有有限基且其生成函数为代数生成函数。

Conclusion: Cayley排列的替换分解理论成功推广了置换中的相应框架，为相关遗传类的有限基与代数生成函数性质提供了统一理论依据。

Abstract: We propose a notion of simplicity for Cayley permutations that is compatible with inflation. We prove that Cayley permutations admit a substitution decomposition analogous to that of permutations and use it to enumerate simple Cayley permutations, primitive simple Cayley permutations, and simple restricted growth functions. We also prove that every hereditary Cayley permutation class with finitely many simple members has a finite basis and an algebraic generating function.

</details>


### [159] [Hamiltonicity in graphs defined by primes and primitive elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19114)
*Yue-Feng She*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于所有足够大的n，2n阶素数圆圈均存在；并研究了有限域上由本原和与本原差定义的图与有向图中的哈密顿圈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素数圆圈问题是组合数论中的经典问题，此前仅对特定范围或借助计算手段验证；作者希望从根本上解决大n情形的存在性，并推广到有限域上由本原和/差构造的图中哈密顿圈问题。

Method: 证明基于完美匹配（perfect matching）与鲁棒扩张（robust expansion）技术；进一步研究有限域F_q上的本原和图与本原差图，利用扩张性质与匹配方法证明哈密顿性。

Result: 证明了每个足够大的n都存在2n阶素数圆圈；F_q上的本原和图对每个素数幂q>18,888,871都是哈密顿的，而在完全素域F_p上界改进为p>61。

Conclusion: 素数圆圈问题在大n范围内得到解决，所发展的匹配与扩张方法也给出了有限域上本原和/差图哈密顿性的强结论，并给出具体且相对小的界。

Abstract: A prime circle of order $2n$ is a circular ordering of $1,\ldots,2n$ such that the sum of every two adjacent terms is prime. We prove that a prime circle exists for every sufficiently large $n$. The proof is based on a perfect matching and robust expansion. We also study Hamilton cycles in graphs and digraphs defined by primitive sums and differences over finite fields. In particular, the primitive-sum graph on $\F_q$ is Hamiltonian for every prime power $q>18\,888\,871$, and for the graph on a full prime field $\F_p$, the bound improves to $p>61$.

</details>
